import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/app/bloc/app_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/app/view/app_view.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/presentation/cubit/verification_cubit.dart';
import 'package:firebase_core/firebase_core.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_router.dart'; // <-- 1. IMPORT NEW ROUTER

Future<void> main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
  await Firebase.initializeApp();
  await configureDependencies();

  // 2. Create the AppBloc *first*
  final appBloc = getIt<AppBloc>()
    ..add(const AppEvent.checkAuthStatus());

  // 3. Create the AppRouter and pass the bloc to it
  final appRouter = AppRouter(appBloc);

  runApp(
    MultiBlocProvider(
      providers: [
        // 4. Provide the *same instance* of AppBloc
        BlocProvider.value(value: appBloc),
        BlocProvider(
          create: (context) => getIt<VerificationCubit>()
            ..fetchStatus(),
        ),
      ],
      // 5. Pass the generated GoRouter instance to AppView
      child: AppView(router: appRouter.router),
    ),
  );
}

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/services/secure_storage_service.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/usecases/get_my_profile.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/entities/user.dart';
part 'app_bloc.freezed.dart';
part 'app_event.dart';
part 'app_state.dart';

@injectable
class AppBloc extends Bloc<AppEvent, AppState> {
  final SecureStorageService _storageService;
  final GetMyProfile _getMyProfile; // <-- ADDED

  AppBloc(this._storageService, this._getMyProfile) // <-- UPDATED CONSTRUCTOR
      : super(const AppState.initial()) {
    on<_CheckAuthStatus>(_onCheckAuthStatus);
    on<_LogoutRequested>(_onLogoutRequested);
  }

  /// Handles the [CheckAuthStatus] event.
  /// Reads the token from secure storage and emits [Authenticated] or
  /// [Unauthenticated] accordingly.
  Future<void> _onCheckAuthStatus(
    _CheckAuthStatus event,
    Emitter<AppState> emit,
  ) async {
    // You could emit a loading state here if needed
    // emit(const AppState.loading());
    try {
      final token = await _storageService.readToken();
      if (token != null) {
        // --- THIS IS THE NEW LOGIC ---
        final user = await _getMyProfile(NoParams());
        emit(AppState.authenticated(user));
        // -----------------------------
      } else {
        emit(const AppState.unauthenticated());
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // If getting profile fails, log them out
      emit(const AppState.unauthenticated());
    }
  }

  /// Handles the [LogoutRequested] event.
  /// Deletes the token from storage and emits [Unauthenticated].
  Future<void> _onLogoutRequested(
    _LogoutRequested event,
    Emitter<AppState> emit,
  ) async {
    try {
      await _storageService.deleteToken();
    } catch (e) {
      // Handle potential storage error, though we still want to log out
      print('Error deleting token: $e');
    }
    emit(const AppState.unauthenticated());
  }
}

part of 'app_bloc.dart';

@freezed
sealed class AppEvent with _$AppEvent {
  /// Dispatched on app startup to check if a valid token exists in storage.
  const factory AppEvent.checkAuthStatus() = _CheckAuthStatus;

  /// Dispatched when the user logs out or a 401 error is received.
  const factory AppEvent.logoutRequested() = _LogoutRequested;
}

@freezed
sealed class AppState with _$AppState {
  /// The initial state before any auth check has been performed.
  const factory AppState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// The state when a user is successfully authenticated (token exists).
  // AFTER
  const factory AppState.authenticated(User user) = _Authenticated;

  /// The state when no user is authenticated (no token).
  const factory AppState.unauthenticated() = _Unauthenticated;
}

// lib/app/view/app_view.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/app/bloc/app_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';

class AppView extends StatelessWidget {
  final GoRouter router;
  
  const AppView({super.key, required this.router});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MaterialApp.router(
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      title: 'SahbiFit',
      theme: AppTheme.lightTheme,

      // Use the routerConfig from the router instance
      routerConfig: router,

      // KEEP THE BUILDER - This is still the best way to
      // handle the initial loading screen flash.
      builder: (context, child) {
        return BlocBuilder<AppBloc, AppState>(
          builder: (context, state) {
            return state.maybeWhen(
              initial: () => const Scaffold(
                backgroundColor: AppTheme.cleanWhite,
                body: Center(
                  child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                    color: AppTheme.primaryRed,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              // Once auth state is known (authenticated/unauthenticated),
              // the redirect logic will have run. We can show the page.
              orElse: () => child!,
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/api/interceptors.dart';

@lazySingleton
class ApiClient {
  final Dio dio;

  ApiClient(AuthInterceptor authInterceptor)
      : dio = Dio(
          BaseOptions(
            // IMPORTANT: Change YOUR_IP to your computer's IP address
            // (e.g., 192.168.1.5) or 'localhost' if using web.
            baseUrl: 'http://YOUR_IP:8080/api', 
            connectTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 10),
            receiveTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 10),
            headers: {
              'Content-Type': 'application/json',
              'Accept': 'application/json',
            },
          ),
        ) {
    // Add the auth interceptor to Dio's interceptor list
    dio.interceptors.add(authInterceptor);

    // You can add other interceptors here, like a logger
    dio.interceptors.add(LogInterceptor(
      requestBody: true,
      responseBody: true,
    ));
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\Ahmed\Desktop\sahbifit\mobile\lib\core\api\interceptors.dart

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/app/bloc/app_bloc.dart'; // <-- 1. Import AppBloc
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart'; // <-- 2. Import getIt
import 'package:sahbifit/core/services/secure_storage_service.dart';

@lazySingleton
class AuthInterceptor extends Interceptor {
  final SecureStorageService _storageService;

  AuthInterceptor(this._storageService);

  @override
  Future<void> onRequest(
    RequestOptions options,
    RequestInterceptorHandler handler,
  ) async {
    // Read the token from storage
    final String? token = await _storageService.readToken();

    // Add the Authorization header if the token exists
    if (token != null) {
      options.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer $token';
    }

    return super.onRequest(options, handler);
  }

  @override
  void onError(DioException err, ErrorInterceptorHandler handler) {
    if (err.response?.statusCode == 401) {
      print('--- 401 Unauthorized: Dispatching LogoutRequested ---');

      // 3. THIS IS THE CHANGE
      // Use getIt to find the globally registered AppBloc
      // and add the logout event. The AppBloc will handle
      // deleting the token and updating the app's state.
      try {
        getIt<AppBloc>().add(const AppEvent.logoutRequested());
      } catch (e) {
        print('Failed to get AppBloc from getIt to dispatch logout: $e');
      }
    }
    return super.onError(err, handler);
  }
}

/// A wrapper class to hold the GoRouter instance.
/// This allows us to pass the AppBloc to the redirect logic.
class AppRouter {
  final AppBloc appBloc;
  late final GoRouter router;

  AppRouter(this.appBloc) {
    router = GoRouter(
      // 1. Listen to the AppBloc stream for auth changes
      refreshListenable: GoRouterRefreshStream(appBloc.stream),

      // 2. Set the initial route
      initialLocation: '/login',

      // 3. Define the auth redirect logic
      redirect: (BuildContext context, GoRouterState state) {
        final appState = appBloc.state;
        final location = state.matchedLocation;

        // Define auth-only routes
        final authRoutes = [
          '/login',
          '/register',
          '/verify-sms',
        ];

        // If the app is still checking auth, don't redirect.
        // The AppView's builder will show a loading screen.
        if (appState is _Initial) {
          return null;
        }

        // Handle unauthenticated users
        if (appState is _Unauthenticated) {
          // If the user is on an auth page, allow them.
          if (authRoutes.contains(location)) {
            return null;
          }
          // Otherwise, redirect to login.
          return '/login';
        }

        // Handle authenticated users
        if (appState is _Authenticated) {
          // If the user is on an auth page, redirect to home.
          if (authRoutes.contains(location)) {
            return '/home';
          }
          // Otherwise, allow them to go anywhere.
          return null;
        }

        return null; // Default case
      },

      // 4. Define all your app routes
      routes: [
        // --- Auth Flow ---
        GoRoute(
          path: '/login',
          builder: (context, state) => const LoginPage(),
        ),
        GoRoute(
          path: '/register',
          builder: (context, state) => const RegisterPage(),
        ),
        GoRoute(
          path: '/verify-sms',
          builder: (context, state) {
            // Get arguments passed via `extra`
            final phoneNumber = state.extra as String;
            return VerifySmsPage(phoneNumber: phoneNumber);
          },
        ),

        // --- Main App (Authenticated) ---
        GoRoute(
          path: '/home',
          builder: (context, state) => const SessionFeedPage(),
        ),
        GoRoute(
          path: '/create-session',
          builder: (context, state) => const CreateSessionPage(),
        ),

        // --- Nested Session Routes ---
        GoRoute(
          path: '/session/:sessionId',
          builder: (context, state) {
            // Get path parameters
            final sessionId = state.pathParameters['sessionId']!;
            return SessionDetailsPage(sessionId: sessionId);
          },
          routes: [
            // Path becomes: /session/:sessionId/chat
            GoRoute(
              path: 'chat',
              builder: (context, state) {
                final sessionId = state.pathParameters['sessionId']!;
                return ChatPage(sessionId: sessionId);
              },
            ),
            // Path becomes: /session/:sessionId/attendance
            GoRoute(
              path: 'attendance',
              builder: (context, state) {
                final sessionId = state.pathParameters['sessionId']!;
                return MarkAttendancePage(sessionId: sessionId);
              },
            ),
          ],
        ),

        // --- User & Profile ---
        GoRoute(
          path: '/profile', // My profile
          builder: (context, state) => const ProfilePage(userId: null),
        ),
        GoRoute(
          path: '/user/:userId', // Other user's profile
          builder: (context, state) {
            final userId = state.pathParameters['userId']!;
            return ProfilePage(userId: userId);
          },
        ),
        GoRoute(
          path: '/settings',
          builder: (context, state) => const SettingsPage(),
        ),

        // --- Trust & Safety ---
        GoRoute(
          path: '/submit-verification',
          builder: (context, state) => const SubmitVerificationPage(),
        ),
        GoRoute(
          path: '/report-user',
          builder: (context, state) {
            // Arguments can be passed via `extra` as a Map or custom class
            final args = state.extra as Map<String, dynamic>;
            return ReportUserPage(
              reportedUserId: args['reportedUserId'] as String,
              sessionId: args['sessionId'] as String?,
            );
          },
        ),

        // --- Other Features ---
        GoRoute(
          path: '/pending-ratings',
          builder: (context, state) => const PendingRatingsPage(),
        ),
        GoRoute(
          path: '/submit-gym',
          builder: (context, state) => const SubmitGymPage(),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// A simple [ChangeNotifier] that listens to a [Stream]
/// and notifies listeners on new events.
class GoRouterRefreshStream extends ChangeNotifier {
  GoRouterRefreshStream(Stream<dynamic> stream) {
    notifyListeners();
    _subscription = stream.asBroadcastStream().listen(
          (dynamic _) => notifyListeners(),
        );
  }

  late final StreamSubscription<dynamic> _subscription;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _subscription.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';

class ServerException implements Exception {
  final String message;

  ServerException({required this.message});

  /// This factory constructor creates a [ServerException] from a [DioException].
  /// It parses the Dio error to find the most helpful message.
  factory ServerException.fromDio(DioException e) {
    String message = 'An unknown server error occurred.';

    // Try to get a specific error message from the backend response
    if (e.response != null && e.response?.data != null) {
      // Check if the backend sent a map with a 'message' key
      // e.g., { "message": "Phone number is already registered." }
      if (e.response?.data is Map) {
        message = e.response?.data['message'] ?? message;
      }
      // Check if the backend just sent a plain text error
      else if (e.response?.data is String) {
        final responseString = e.response!.data as String;
        if (responseString.isNotEmpty) {
          message = responseString;
        }
      }
    } 
    // Handle common Dio error types
    else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout ||
        e.type == DioExceptionType.receiveTimeout ||
        e.type == DioExceptionType.sendTimeout) {
      message = 'Connection timed out. Please try again.';
    } else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.connectionError) {
      message = 'Network error. Please check your internet connection.';
    } else if (e.type == DioExceptionType.cancel) {
      message = 'Request was cancelled.';
    }

    return ServerException(message: message);
  }
}

/// Thrown if the user is not authorized (e.g., 401 error).
/// Our interceptor might catch this first, but it's good to have.
class UnauthorizedException implements Exception {
  final String message;
  UnauthorizedException({this.message = 'You are not authorized.'});
}

/// Thrown when dealing with local data storage.
class CacheException implements Exception {}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';

/// A [Failure] is an error that is safe to show to the user
/// and will be handled by the UI/Presentation layer.
abstract class Failure extends Equatable {
  final String message;

  const Failure({required this.message});

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

/// Represents a failure from the server (e.g., 4xx, 5xx errors).
/// This is what we used in our [AuthRepositoryImpl].
class ServerFailure extends Failure {
  const ServerFailure({required super.message});
}

/// Represents a failure when accessing local cache.
class CacheFailure extends Failure {
  const CacheFailure({super.message = 'Error reading from local storage.'});
}

/// Represents a failure when there is no network connection.
class NetworkFailure extends Failure {
  const NetworkFailure({super.message = 'Please check your internet connection.'});
}

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:firebase_core/firebase_core.dart';
import 'package:firebase_messaging/firebase_messaging.dart';
import 'package:flutter_local_notifications/flutter_local_notifications.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_router.dart'; // Import your AppRouter
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/repositories/user_repository.dart';

// This must be a top-level function (not part of a class)
@pragma('vm:entry-point')
Future<void> _firebaseMessagingBackgroundHandler(RemoteMessage message) async {
  // If you're going to use other services in the background, make sure
  // to initialize them first, e.g., await Firebase.initializeApp().
  await Firebase.initializeApp();
  print("--- Handling a background message: ${message.messageId}");
  // You can't do navigation here, but you can process data
}

@lazySingleton
class PushNotificationService {
  final FirebaseMessaging _fcm = FirebaseMessaging.instance;
  final UserRepository _userRepository; // To send the token to the backend
  final FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin _localNotifications =
      FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin();

  PushNotificationService(this._userRepository);

  Future<void> init() async {
    // 1. Request permissions (iOS/macOS)
    await _requestPermissions();

    // 2. Initialize local notifications for foreground display
    await _initLocalNotifications();

    // 3. Get the FCM token and send it to the backend
    await _initToken();

    // 4. Set up message handlers
    _setupMessageHandlers();
  }

  Future<void> _requestPermissions() async {
    if (Platform.isIOS || Platform.isMacOS) {
      await _fcm.requestPermission(
        alert: true,
        badge: true,
        sound: true,
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _initToken() async {
    try {
      final String? token = await _fcm.getToken();
      if (token != null) {
        print("--- FCM Token: $token ---");
        // Send the token to your backend
        // This will call the usecase which calls the repository
        // which calls the datasource to hit PUT /api/users/me/fcm-token
        await _userRepository.updateFcmToken(token);
      }

      // Listen for token refreshes
      _fcm.onTokenRefresh.listen((newToken) async {
        print("--- FCM Token Refreshed: $newToken ---");
        await _userRepository.updateFcmToken(newToken);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      print("Error getting/sending FCM token: $e");
    }
  }

  Future<void> _initLocalNotifications() async {
    const AndroidInitializationSettings androidSettings =
        AndroidInitializationSettings('@mipmap/ic_launcher');
    const DarwinInitializationSettings iosSettings =
        DarwinInitializationSettings();

    const InitializationSettings settings = InitializationSettings(
      android: androidSettings,
      iOS: iosSettings,
    );

    await _localNotifications.initialize(
      settings,
      onDidReceiveNotificationResponse: (NotificationResponse response) {
        // Handle notification tap when app is in foreground
        if (response.payload != null && response.payload!.isNotEmpty) {
          final data = jsonDecode(response.payload!) as Map<String, dynamic>;
          _handleNotificationTap(data);
        }
      },
    );
  }

  void _setupMessageHandlers() {
    // 1. App is in the FOREGROUND
    FirebaseMessaging.onMessage.listen((RemoteMessage message) {
      print("--- FCM Message (Foreground): ${message.notification?.title}");

      final RemoteNotification? notification = message.notification;
      final AndroidNotification? android = message.notification?.android;

      // Show a local notification
      if (notification != null) {
        _localNotifications.show(
          notification.hashCode,
          notification.title,
          notification.body,
          NotificationDetails(
            android: android != null
                ? AndroidNotificationDetails(
                    'sahbifit_channel_id', // Needs setup on Android
                    'SahbiFit Notifications',
                    channelDescription: 'Main notification channel',
                    importance: Importance.max,
                    priority: Priority.high,
                  )
                : null,
            iOS: const DarwinNotificationDetails(presentSound: true),
          ),
          // Pass the data payload for tap handling
          payload: jsonEncode(message.data),
        );
      }
    });

    // 2. App is in the BACKGROUND (but not terminated) and user taps notification
    FirebaseMessaging.onMessageOpenedApp.listen((RemoteMessage message) {
      print("--- FCM Message (OpenedApp): ${message.data['type']}");
      _handleNotificationTap(message.data);
    });

    // 3. App is TERMINATED and user taps notification
    _fcm.getInitialMessage().then((RemoteMessage? message) {
      if (message != null) {
        print("--- FCM Message (Initial): ${message.data['type']}");
        _handleNotificationTap(message.data);
      }
    });

    // 4. Background message handler
    FirebaseMessaging.onBackgroundMessage(_firebaseMessagingBackgroundHandler);
  }

  /// This is the main routing logic based on your backend's data payload.
  void _handleNotificationTap(Map<String, dynamic> data) {
    final String? type = data['type'];
    if (type == null) return;

    // Use getIt to find the AppRouter
    final appRouter = getIt<AppRouter>();

    switch (type) {
      case 'VERIFICATION_APPROVED':
      case 'VERIFICATION_REJECTED':
        // Navigate to the user's profile
        appRouter.push(const ProfileRoute());
        break;

      case 'PENDING_RATING':
        // This matches 'sendRatingReminder' from your backend
        final String? sessionId = data['sessionId'];
        if (sessionId != null) {
          // Navigate to the pending ratings page
          appRouter.push(const PendingRatingsRoute());
        }
        break;

      default:
        // Default action, e.g., go to home
        appRouter.push(const SessionFeedRoute());
    }
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:recaptcha_v3/recaptcha_v3.dart';

@lazySingleton
class ReCaptchaService {
  // IMPORTANT: Replace with your actual reCAPTCHA v3 Site Key
  // You should fetch this from your environment variables/config
  static const String _siteKey = 'YOUR_RECAPTCHA_V3_SITE_KEY';
  bool _isInitialized = false;

  Future<void> init() async {
    // We only need to initialize on Web
    if (kIsWeb) {
      try {
        await RecaptchaV3.initialize(_siteKey);
        _isInitialized = true;
        debugPrint("reCAPTCHA v3 initialized.");
      } catch (e) {
        debugPrint("Error initializing reCAPTCHA v3: $e");
      }
    }
  }

  /// Gets a reCAPTCHA v3 token for a specific action.
  ///
  /// Your backend expects actions like 'register' or 'resend-otp'.
  /// Returns a dummy token on mobile (as v3 is web-focused)
  /// and a real token on web.
  Future<String> getToken(String action) async {
    if (kIsWeb) {
      if (!_isInitialized) {
        debugPrint("reCAPTCHA not initialized, attempting to init now...");
        await init();
        if (!_isInitialized) {
          debugPrint("reCAPTCHA v3 failed to initialize.");
          throw Exception("reCAPTCHA v3 failed to initialize.");
        }
      }
      try {
        final String token = await RecaptchaV3.execute(action);
        debugPrint("reCAPTCHA v3 token for action '$action': $token");
        return token;
      } catch (e) {
        debugPrint("Error executing reCAPTCHA v3 action '$action': $e");
        throw Exception("Failed to get reCAPTCHA token.");
      }
    } else {
      // reCAPTCHA v3 is primarily for web.
      // For mobile, you might use Google Play Integrity or App Check.
      // For now, we return a "dummy" token that your backend
      // *should* be configured to accept during development/testing.
      debugPrint("--- MOCK reCAPTCHA: Returning dummy token for mobile ---");
      return 'MOBILE_DUMMY_TOKEN_FOR_TESTING';
    }
  }
}

import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';

@lazySingleton
class SecureStorageService {
  final FlutterSecureStorage _storage;

  // Use const for keys
  static const String _keyToken = 'jwt_token';

  SecureStorageService(this._storage);

  Future<void> writeToken(String token) async {
    await _storage.write(key: _keyToken, value: token);
  }

  Future<String?> readToken() async {
    return await _storage.read(key: _keyToken);
}

  Future<void> deleteToken() async {
    await _storage.delete(key: _keyToken);
  }
}

// We need to tell GetIt how to create FlutterSecureStorage
@module
abstract class StorageModule {
  @lazySingleton
  FlutterSecureStorage get storage => const FlutterSecureStorage();
}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';

/// [Type] is the return type of the usecase (e.g., AuthToken, void).
/// [Params] is the parameters class (e.g., LoginParams).
abstract class UseCase<Type, Params> {
  /// The [call] method is the single entry point for the usecase.
  /// It's designed to be asynchronous and return either a [Failure]
  /// or the expected [Type].
  ///
  /// Note: In our Auth implementation, we are letting exceptions bubble up
  /// to the BLoC. You can also use an Either<Failure, Type> wrapper here.
  /// For simplicity, we'll stick to throwing Failures.
  Future<Type> call(Params params);
}

/// A helper class for usecases that do not require any parameters.
/// For example: `GetMyProfile(NoParams())`
class NoParams extends Equatable {
  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

/// Formats a DateTime object into the 'YYYY-MM-DD' string
/// required by your backend's `RegisterRequest`.
String formatToYyyyMmDd(DateTime date) {
  return DateFormat('yyyy-MM-dd').format(date);
}

/// Formats an ISO 8601 string (like those from OffsetDateTime)
/// into a more readable format for the UI.
///
/// Example: "2025-11-13T16:04:39+01:00" -> "Nov 13, 2025, 4:04 PM"
String formatReadableDateTime(String isoString) {
  try {
    final DateTime dateTime = DateTime.parse(isoString);
    // Format: "MMM d, yyyy, h:mm a" (e.g., "Nov 13, 2025, 4:04 PM")
    return DateFormat.yMd().add_jm().format(dateTime.toLocal());
  } catch (e) {
    print("Error formatting date: $e");
    return "Invalid Date";
  }
}

/// Formats just the time from an ISO 8601 string.
///
/// Example: "2025-11-13T16:04:39+01:00" -> "4:04 PM"
String formatReadableTime(String isoString) {
   try {
    final DateTime dateTime = DateTime.parse(isoString);
    // Format: "h:mm a" (e.g., "4:04 PM")
    return DateFormat.jm().format(dateTime.toLocal());
  } catch (e) {
    print("Error formatting time: $e");
    return "--:--";
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/enums/session_type.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/enums/verified_gender_enum.dart';


// Assuming your enums are defined in your domain models.
// Example: enum SessionType { group, one_on_one }
// Example: enum VerifiedGender { not_verified, male, female, other }

/// Returns an icon based on the session type.
IconData getSessionTypeIcon(SessionType type) {
  switch (type) {
    case SessionType.group:
      return Icons.groups_rounded;
    case SessionType.oneOnOne:
      return Icons.person_rounded;
  }
}

/// Returns an icon based on the user's verified gender.
IconData getGenderIcon(VerifiedGender gender) {
  switch (gender) {
    case VerifiedGender.male:
      return Icons.male_rounded;
    case VerifiedGender.female:
      return Icons.female_rounded;
    case VerifiedGender.other:
      return Icons.transgender_rounded;
    case VerifiedGender.notVerified:
      return Icons.help_outline_rounded;
  }
}

/// Validates a phone number based on the backend's Tunisian number regex.
///
/// Regex: `^(2|3|4|5|7|9)\d{7}$`
/// (Must start with 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, or 9, followed by 7 digits).
String? validateTunisianPhoneNumber(String? value) {
  if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
    return 'Phone number cannot be empty.';
  }
  
  // This regex matches your Java backend's logic
  final RegExp tunisianPhoneRegex = RegExp(r'^(2|3|4|5|7|9)\d{7}$');
  
  if (!tunisianPhoneRegex.hasMatch(value)) {
    return 'Invalid Tunisian phone number (8 digits, no +216).';
  }
  
  return null; // Valid
}

import 'package:dartz/dartz.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';

/// A type definition for a Future that returns either a Failure or a Type [T].
/// This is commonly used in repositories and use cases.
///
/// Example: `FutureEither<User> getMyProfile()`
typedef FutureEither<T> = Future<Either<Failure, T>>;

/// A type definition for a Future that returns either a Failure or void.
/// Used for operations that don't return data, like `deleteToken()`.
///
/// Example: `FutureVoid deleteToken()`
typedef FutureVoid = FutureEither<void>;

/// A common type definition for JSON data.
///
/// Example: `DataMap json = response.data as DataMap;`
typedef DataMap = Map<String, dynamic>;

/// A generic validator for non-empty text fields.
String? validateNotEmpty(String? value, String fieldName) {
  if (value == null || value.trim().isEmpty) {
    return '$fieldName cannot be empty.';
  }
  return null; // Valid
}

/// Validates the password based on your backend's `RegisterRequest`.
/// (`@Size(min = 8, message = "Password must be at least 8 characters long.")`)
String? validatePassword(String? value) {
  if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
    return 'Password cannot be empty.';
  }
  if (value.length < 8) {
    return 'Password must be at least 8 characters long.';
  }
  return null; // Valid
}

/// Validates the OTP code based on your backend's `VerifySmsRequest`.
/// (`@Size(min = 6, max = 6, message = "OTP must be 6 digits long.")`)
String? validateOtp(String? value) {
  if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
    return 'OTP cannot be empty.';
  }
  if (value.length != 6) {
    return 'OTP must be 6 digits long.';
  }
  // Optional: Check if all characters are digits
  if (int.tryParse(value) == null) {
    return 'OTP must only contain numbers.';
  }
  return null; // Valid
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/api/api_client.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/constants/api_endpoints.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/auth_response_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/login_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/register_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/resend_otp_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/verify_sms_request_model.dart';

// This is the abstract interface
abstract class AuthRemoteDatasource {
  Future<AuthResponseModel> login(LoginRequestModel request);
  Future<void> register(RegisterRequestModel request);
  Future<void> verifySms(VerifySmsRequestModel request);
  Future<void> resendOtp(ResendOtpRequestModel request);
}

@Injectable(as: AuthRemoteDatasource)
class AuthRemoteDatasourceImpl implements AuthRemoteDatasource {
  final ApiClient _apiClient;

  AuthRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._apiClient);

  @override
  Future<AuthResponseModel> login(LoginRequestModel request) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.login, // <-- USE CONSTANT
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
      return AuthResponseModel.fromJson(response.data);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> register(RegisterRequestModel request) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.register, // <-- USE CONSTANT
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> verifySms(VerifySmsRequestModel request) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.verifySms, // <-- USE CONSTANT
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> resendOtp(ResendOtpRequestModel request) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.resendOtp, // <-- USE CONSTANT
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/entities/auth_token.dart';

part 'auth_response_model.freezed.dart';
part 'auth_response_model.g.dart';


@freezed
abstract class AuthResponseModel with _$AuthResponseModel {
  const AuthResponseModel._(); // Private constructor for methods

  const factory AuthResponseModel({
    required String jwtToken,
  }) = _AuthResponseModel;

  factory AuthResponseModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$AuthResponseModelFromJson(json);

  // Converts the data-layer model to a domain-layer entity
  AuthToken toEntity() {
    return AuthToken(token: jwtToken);
  }
}

/// @nodoc
@JsonSerializable()

class _AuthResponseModel extends AuthResponseModel {
  const _AuthResponseModel({required this.jwtToken}): super._();
  factory _AuthResponseModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => _$AuthResponseModelFromJson(json);

@override final  String jwtToken;

/// Create a copy of AuthResponseModel
/// with the given fields replaced by the non-null parameter values.

import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/services/secure_storage_service.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/datasources/auth_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/login_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/register_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/resend_otp_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/verify_sms_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/entities/auth_token.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/repositories/auth_repository.dart';

@Injectable(as: AuthRepository)
class AuthRepositoryImpl implements AuthRepository {
  final AuthRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;
  final SecureStorageService _storageService;

  AuthRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource, this._storageService);

  @override
  Future<AuthToken> login(String phoneNumber, String password) async {
    try {
      final requestModel = LoginRequestModel(
        phoneNumber: phoneNumber,
        password: password,
      );
      final responseModel = await _remoteDatasource.login(requestModel);

      // --- 
      // ðŸ’¡ KEY REQUIREMENT: Write the token to secure storage
      // ---
      await _storageService.writeToken(responseModel.jwtToken);
      
      return responseModel.toEntity();
    } on ServerException catch(e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> register(RegisterRequestModel request) async {
    try {
      await _remoteDatasource.register(request);
    } on ServerException catch(e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> verifySms(String phoneNumber, String otp) async {
    try {
      final requestModel = VerifySmsRequestModel(
        phoneNumber: phoneNumber,
        otp: otp,
      );
      await _remoteDatasource.verifySms(requestModel);
    } on ServerException catch(e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
    
  @override
  Future<void> resendOtp(String phoneNumber, String recaptchaToken) async {
    try {
      final requestModel = ResendOtpRequestModel(
        phoneNumber: phoneNumber,
        recaptchaToken: recaptchaToken,
      );
      await _remoteDatasource.resendOtp(requestModel);
    } on ServerException catch(e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/register_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/entities/auth_token.dart';

// Using a pattern that throws a Failure on error
abstract class AuthRepository {
  Future<AuthToken> login(String phoneNumber, String password);
  Future<void> register(RegisterRequestModel request);
  Future<void> verifySms(String phoneNumber, String otp);
  Future<void> resendOtp(String phoneNumber, String recaptchaToken);
}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/entities/auth_token.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/repositories/auth_repository.dart';

@injectable
class LoginUser implements UseCase<AuthToken, LoginParams> {
  final AuthRepository _repository;

  LoginUser(this._repository);

  @override
  Future<AuthToken> call(LoginParams params) async {
    return _repository.login(params.phoneNumber, params.password);
  }
}

class LoginParams extends Equatable {
  final String phoneNumber;
  final String password;

  const LoginParams({required this.phoneNumber, required this.password});

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [phoneNumber, password];
}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/data/models/register_request_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/repositories/auth_repository.dart';

@injectable
class RegisterUser implements UseCase<void, RegisterParams> {
  final AuthRepository _repository;

  RegisterUser(this._repository);

  @override
  Future<void> call(RegisterParams params) async {
    // The Usecase maps the simple params into the data model
    final requestModel = RegisterRequestModel(
      phoneNumber: params.phoneNumber,
      password: params.password,
      recaptchaToken: params.recaptchaToken,
      firstName: params.firstName,
      dateOfBirth: params.dateOfBirth,
      primaryGymId: params.primaryGymId,
      goalTagIds: params.goalTagIds,
      scheduleTagIds: params.scheduleTagIds,
      profilePhotoUrl: params.profilePhotoUrl,
    );
    return _repository.register(requestModel);
  }
}

class RegisterParams extends Equatable {
  final String phoneNumber;
  final String password;
  final String recaptchaToken;
  final String firstName;
  final String dateOfBirth;
  final String primaryGymId;
  final List<int> goalTagIds;
  final List<int> scheduleTagIds;
  final String? profilePhotoUrl;

  const RegisterParams({
    required this.phoneNumber,
    required this.password,
    required this.recaptchaToken,
    required this.firstName,
    required this.dateOfBirth,
    required this.primaryGymId,
    required this.goalTagIds,
    required this.scheduleTagIds,
    this.profilePhotoUrl,
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
        phoneNumber,
        password,
        recaptchaToken,
        firstName,
        dateOfBirth,
        primaryGymId,
        goalTagIds,
        scheduleTagIds,
        profilePhotoUrl,
      ];
}

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';

import 'package:sahbifit/app/bloc/app_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/usecases/login_user.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/usecases/register_user.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/usecases/resend_otp.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/usecases/verify_sms.dart';

part 'auth_bloc.freezed.dart';
part 'auth_event.dart';
part 'auth_state.dart';

@injectable
class AuthBloc extends Bloc<AuthEvent, AuthState> {
  final LoginUser _loginUser;
  final RegisterUser _registerUser;
  final VerifySms _verifySms;
  final ResendOtp _resendOtp;
  final AppBloc _appBloc; // Injected to dispatch events to the AppBloc

  AuthBloc(
    this._loginUser,
    this._registerUser,
    this._verifySms,
    this._resendOtp,
    this._appBloc,
  ) : super(const AuthState.initial()) {
    on<_LoginButtonPressed>(_onLoginButtonPressed);
    on<_RegisterButtonPressed>(_onRegisterButtonPressed);
    on<_VerifySmsButtonPressed>(_onVerifySmsButtonPressed);
    on<_ResendOtpButtonPressed>(_onResendOtpButtonPressed);
  }

  Future<void> _onLoginButtonPressed(
    _LoginButtonPressed event,
    Emitter<AuthState> emit,
  ) async {
    emit(const AuthState.loading());
    try {
      final params = LoginParams(
        phoneNumber: event.phoneNumber,
        password: event.password,
      );
      await _loginUser(params);

      // ---
      // ðŸš€ REQUIREMENT FULFILLED
      // On success, dispatch CheckAuthStatus to the AppBloc.
      // The AppBloc will read the new token and emit AppState.authenticated
      // ---
      _appBloc.add(const AppEvent.checkAuthStatus());

      emit(const AuthState.loginSuccess());
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(AuthState.failure(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(AuthState.failure(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _onRegisterButtonPressed(
    _RegisterButtonPressed event,
    Emitter<AuthState> emit,
  ) async {
    emit(const AuthState.loading());
    try {
      // The event already contains the complex RegisterParams
      await _registerUser(event.params);
      emit(AuthState.registrationSuccess(
        phoneNumber: event.params.phoneNumber,
      ));
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(AuthState.failure(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(AuthState.failure(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _onVerifySmsButtonPressed(
    _VerifySmsButtonPressed event,
    Emitter<AuthState> emit,
  ) async {
    emit(const AuthState.loading());
    try {
      final params = VerifySmsParams(
        phoneNumber: event.phoneNumber,
        otp: event.otp,
      );
      await _verifySms(params);
      emit(const AuthState.verificationSuccess());
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(AuthState.failure(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(AuthState.failure(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _onResendOtpButtonPressed(
    _ResendOtpButtonPressed event,
    Emitter<AuthState> emit,
  ) async {
    emit(const AuthState.loading());
    try {
      final params = ResendOtpParams(
        phoneNumber: event.phoneNumber,
        recaptchaToken: event.recaptchaToken,
      );
      await _resendOtp(params);
      emit(const AuthState.otpResent());
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(AuthState.failure(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(AuthState.failure(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

/// Adds pattern-matching-related methods to [AuthState].
extension AuthStatePatterns on AuthState {
/// A variant of `map` that fallback to returning `orElse`.
///
/// It is equivalent to doing:
/// ```dart
/// switch (sealedClass) {
///   case final Subclass value:
///     return ...;
///   case _:
///     return orElse();
/// }
/// ```

@optionalTypeArgs TResult maybeMap<TResult extends Object?>({TResult Function( _Initial value)?  initial,TResult Function( _Loading value)?  loading,TResult Function( _LoginSuccess value)?  loginSuccess,TResult Function( _RegistrationSuccess value)?  registrationSuccess,TResult Function( _VerificationSuccess value)?  verificationSuccess,TResult Function( _OtpResent value)?  otpResent,TResult Function( _Failure value)?  failure,required TResult orElse(),}){
final _that = this;
switch (_that) {
case _Initial() when initial != null:
return initial(_that);case _Loading() when loading != null:
return loading(_that);case _LoginSuccess() when loginSuccess != null:
return loginSuccess(_that);case _RegistrationSuccess() when registrationSuccess != null:
return registrationSuccess(_that);case _VerificationSuccess() when verificationSuccess != null:
return verificationSuccess(_that);case _OtpResent() when otpResent != null:
return otpResent(_that);case _Failure() when failure != null:
return failure(_that);case _:
  return orElse();

}
}
/// A `switch`-like method, using callbacks.
///
/// Callbacks receives the raw object, upcasted.
/// It is equivalent to doing:
/// ```dart
/// switch (sealedClass) {
///   case final Subclass value:
///     return ...;
///   case final Subclass2 value:
///     return ...;
/// }
/// ```

@optionalTypeArgs TResult map<TResult extends Object?>({required TResult Function( _Initial value)  initial,required TResult Function( _Loading value)  loading,required TResult Function( _LoginSuccess value)  loginSuccess,required TResult Function( _RegistrationSuccess value)  registrationSuccess,required TResult Function( _VerificationSuccess value)  verificationSuccess,required TResult Function( _OtpResent value)  otpResent,required TResult Function( _Failure value)  failure,}){
final _that = this;
switch (_that) {
case _Initial():
return initial(_that);case _Loading():
return loading(_that);case _LoginSuccess():
return loginSuccess(_that);case _RegistrationSuccess():
return registrationSuccess(_that);case _VerificationSuccess():
return verificationSuccess(_that);case _OtpResent():
return otpResent(_that);case _Failure():
return failure(_that);}
}
/// A variant of `map` that fallback to returning `null`.
///
/// It is equivalent to doing:
/// ```dart
/// switch (sealedClass) {
///   case final Subclass value:
///     return ...;
///   case _:
///     return null;
/// }
/// ```

@optionalTypeArgs TResult? mapOrNull<TResult extends Object?>({TResult? Function( _Initial value)?  initial,TResult? Function( _Loading value)?  loading,TResult? Function( _LoginSuccess value)?  loginSuccess,TResult? Function( _RegistrationSuccess value)?  registrationSuccess,TResult? Function( _VerificationSuccess value)?  verificationSuccess,TResult? Function( _OtpResent value)?  otpResent,TResult? Function( _Failure value)?  failure,}){
final _that = this;
switch (_that) {
case _Initial() when initial != null:
return initial(_that);case _Loading() when loading != null:
return loading(_that);case _LoginSuccess() when loginSuccess != null:
return loginSuccess(_that);case _RegistrationSuccess() when registrationSuccess != null:
return registrationSuccess(_that);case _VerificationSuccess() when verificationSuccess != null:
return verificationSuccess(_that);case _OtpResent() when otpResent != null:
return otpResent(_that);case _Failure() when failure != null:
return failure(_that);case _:
  return null;

}
}
/// A variant of `when` that fallback to an `orElse` callback.
///
/// It is equivalent to doing:
/// ```dart
/// switch (sealedClass) {
///   case Subclass(:final field):
///     return ...;
///   case _:
///     return orElse();
/// }
/// ```

@optionalTypeArgs TResult maybeWhen<TResult extends Object?>({TResult Function()?  initial,TResult Function()?  loading,TResult Function()?  loginSuccess,TResult Function( String phoneNumber)?  registrationSuccess,TResult Function()?  verificationSuccess,TResult Function()?  otpResent,TResult Function( String message)?  failure,required TResult orElse(),}) {final _that = this;
switch (_that) {
case _Initial() when initial != null:
return initial();case _Loading() when loading != null:
return loading();case _LoginSuccess() when loginSuccess != null:
return loginSuccess();case _RegistrationSuccess() when registrationSuccess != null:
return registrationSuccess(_that.phoneNumber);case _VerificationSuccess() when verificationSuccess != null:
return verificationSuccess();case _OtpResent() when otpResent != null:
return otpResent();case _Failure() when failure != null:
return failure(_that.message);case _:
  return orElse();

}
}
/// A `switch`-like method, using callbacks.
///
/// As opposed to `map`, this offers destructuring.
/// It is equivalent to doing:
/// ```dart
/// switch (sealedClass) {
///   case Subclass(:final field):
///     return ...;
///   case Subclass2(:final field2):
///     return ...;
/// }
/// ```

@optionalTypeArgs TResult when<TResult extends Object?>({required TResult Function()  initial,required TResult Function()  loading,required TResult Function()  loginSuccess,required TResult Function( String phoneNumber)  registrationSuccess,required TResult Function()  verificationSuccess,required TResult Function()  otpResent,required TResult Function( String message)  failure,}) {final _that = this;
switch (_that) {
case _Initial():
return initial();case _Loading():
return loading();case _LoginSuccess():
return loginSuccess();case _RegistrationSuccess():
return registrationSuccess(_that.phoneNumber);case _VerificationSuccess():
return verificationSuccess();case _OtpResent():
return otpResent();case _Failure():
return failure(_that.message);}
}
/// A variant of `when` that fallback to returning `null`
///
/// It is equivalent to doing:
/// ```dart
/// switch (sealedClass) {
///   case Subclass(:final field):
///     return ...;
///   case _:
///     return null;
/// }
/// ```

@optionalTypeArgs TResult? whenOrNull<TResult extends Object?>({TResult? Function()?  initial,TResult? Function()?  loading,TResult? Function()?  loginSuccess,TResult? Function( String phoneNumber)?  registrationSuccess,TResult? Function()?  verificationSuccess,TResult? Function()?  otpResent,TResult? Function( String message)?  failure,}) {final _that = this;
switch (_that) {
case _Initial() when initial != null:
return initial();case _Loading() when loading != null:
return loading();case _LoginSuccess() when loginSuccess != null:
return loginSuccess();case _RegistrationSuccess() when registrationSuccess != null:
return registrationSuccess(_that.phoneNumber);case _VerificationSuccess() when verificationSuccess != null:
return verificationSuccess();case _OtpResent() when otpResent != null:
return otpResent();case _Failure() when failure != null:
return failure(_that.message);case _:
  return null;

}
}

}

part of 'auth_bloc.dart';

@freezed
sealed class AuthEvent with _$AuthEvent {
  /// Dispatched when the user presses the login button.
  const factory AuthEvent.loginButtonPressed({
    required String phoneNumber,
    required String password,
  }) = _LoginButtonPressed;

  /// Dispatched when the user presses the final register button.
  /// We pass the complete `RegisterParams` object.
  const factory AuthEvent.registerButtonPressed({
    required RegisterParams params,
  }) = _RegisterButtonPressed;

  /// Dispatched when the user submits the OTP.
  const factory AuthEvent.verifySmsButtonPressed({
    required String phoneNumber,
    required String otp,
  }) = _VerifySmsButtonPressed;

  /// Dispatched when the user requests a new OTP.
  const factory AuthEvent.resendOtpButtonPressed({
    required String phoneNumber,
    required String recaptchaToken,
  }) = _ResendOtpButtonPressed;
}

==================================================
FILE: C:\Users\Ahmed\Desktop\sahbifit\mobile\lib\features\auth\presentation\bloc\auth_state.dart
==================================================
part of 'auth_bloc.dart';

@freezed
sealed class AuthState with _$AuthState {
  /// The default, idle state of the auth forms.
  const factory AuthState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// The state when an auth operation (login, register, etc.) is in progress.
  const factory AuthState.loading() = _Loading;

  /// State emitted upon successful login.
  const factory AuthState.loginSuccess() = _LoginSuccess;

  /// State emitted upon successful registration.
  /// We pass the phone number to the VerifySmsPage.
  const factory AuthState.registrationSuccess({
    required String phoneNumber,
  }) = _RegistrationSuccess;

  /// State emitted upon successful SMS verification.
  const factory AuthState.verificationSuccess() = _VerificationSuccess;

  /// State emitted when a new OTP is successfully sent.
  const factory AuthState.otpResent() = _OtpResent;

  /// The state when an auth operation fails.
  /// The [message] will be displayed to the user.
  const factory AuthState.failure(String message) = _Failure;
}

import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_router.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/presentation/bloc/auth_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/presentation/widgets/login_form.dart';

@RoutePage()
class LoginPage extends StatelessWidget {
  const LoginPage({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocProvider(
      create: (context) => getIt<AuthBloc>(),
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: AppTheme.cleanWhite,
        body: SafeArea(
          child: BlocListener<AuthBloc, AuthState>(
            listener: (context, state) {
              state.maybeWhen(
                // loginSuccess is handled by the AppBloc state change,
                // which will trigger a navigation update.
                // We only need to show errors here.
                failure: (message) {
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    SnackBar(
                      content: Text(message),
                      backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
                    ),
                  );
                },
                orElse: () {},
              );
            },
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppTheme.defaultPadding),
              child: Column(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  // Add your Logo here
                  SizedBox(height: MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * 0.1),
                  Text(
                    'Welcome Back',
                    style: context.textTheme.displaySmall,
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Text(
                    'Login to find your next workout partner.',
                    style: context.textTheme.bodyLarge,
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing * 2),
                  const LoginForm(),
                  const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
                  _buildRegisterButton(context),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRegisterButton(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
      children: [
        Text(
          "Don't have an account?",
          style: context.textTheme.bodyMedium,
        ),
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () {
            // Navigate to the registration page
            context.push(const RegisterRoute());
          },
          child: const Text('Register Now'),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\Ahmed\Desktop\sahbifit\mobile\lib\features\auth\presentation\pages\register_page.dart
==================================================
import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/domain/usecases/register_user.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/presentation/bloc/auth_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/presentation/widgets/profile_setup_form.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/presentation/widgets/register_form_step_1.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/presentation/cubit/gym_list_cubit.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/presentation/cubit/tags_cubit.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/app_bars/custom_app_bar.dart';

@RoutePage()
class RegisterPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const RegisterPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<RegisterPage> createState() => _RegisterPageState();
}

class _RegisterPageState extends State<RegisterPage> {
  final PageController _pageController = PageController();

  // Data from Step 1
  String? _phoneNumber;
  String? _password;
  String? _recaptchaToken;

  void _onStep1Next(
    String phoneNumber,
    String password,
    String recaptchaToken,
  ) {
    setState(() {
      _phoneNumber = phoneNumber;
      _password = password;
      _recaptchaToken = recaptchaToken;
    });
    _pageController.animateToPage(
      1,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
      curve: Curves.easeInOut,
    );
  }

  void _onStep2Register({
    required String firstName,
    required String dateOfBirth,
    required String primaryGymId,
    required List<int> goalTagIds,
    required List<int> scheduleTagIds,
    String? profilePhotoUrl,
  }) {
    if (_phoneNumber == null || _password == null || _recaptchaToken == null) {
      // Should not happen, but as a safeguard
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Error going from step 1 to 2')),
      );
      _pageController.jumpToPage(0);
      return;
    }

    final params = RegisterParams(
      phoneNumber: _phoneNumber!,
      password: _password!,
      recaptchaToken: _recaptchaToken!,
      firstName: firstName,
      dateOfBirth: dateOfBirth,
      primaryGymId: primaryGymId,
      goalTagIds: goalTagIds,
      scheduleTagIds: scheduleTagIds,
      profilePhotoUrl: profilePhotoUrl,
    );

    context.read<AuthBloc>().add(AuthEvent.registerButtonPressed(params: params));
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MultiBlocProvider(
      providers: [
        BlocProvider(create: (context) => getIt<AuthBloc>()),
        BlocProvider(create: (context) => getIt<GymListCubit>()),
        BlocProvider(create: (context) => getIt<TagsCubit>()),
      ],
      child: BlocListener<AuthBloc, AuthState>(
        listener: (context, state) {
          state.maybeWhen(
            registrationSuccess: (phoneNumber) {
              // Navigate to VerifySmsPage on success
              context.push(VerifySmsRoute(phoneNumber: phoneNumber));
            },
            failure: (message) {
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(
                  content: Text(message),
                  backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
                ),
              );
            },
            orElse: () {},
          );
        },
        child: Scaffold(
          appBar: CustomAppBar(
            title: 'Create Account',
            onBackPressed: () {
              if (_pageController.page == 1.0) {
                _pageController.animateToPage(
                  0,
                  duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
                  curve: Curves.easeInOut,
                );
              } else {
                context.router.pop();
              }
            },
          ),
          body: PageView(
            controller: _pageController,
            physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            children: [
              RegisterFormStep1(onNext: _onStep1Next),
              ProfileSetupForm(onRegister: _onStep2Register),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

==================================================
import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_router.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/presentation/bloc/auth_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/presentation/widgets/sms_otp_input.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/app_bars/custom_app_bar.dart';

@RoutePage()
class VerifySmsPage extends StatelessWidget {
  final String phoneNumber;
  const VerifySmsPage({super.key, required this.phoneNumber});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocProvider(
      create: (context) => getIt<AuthBloc>(),
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: const CustomAppBar(title: 'Verify Account'),
        body: BlocListener<AuthBloc, AuthState>(
          listener: (context, state) {
            state.maybeWhen(
              verificationSuccess: () {
                // On success, show a message and pop all auth routes
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  const SnackBar(
                    content: Text('Account verified successfully! Please log in.'),
                    backgroundColor: AppTheme.successGreen,
                  ),
                );
                context.router.popUntil(
                  (route) => route.settings.name == LoginRoute.name,
                );
              },
              otpResent: () {
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  const SnackBar(
                    content: Text('A new OTP has been sent.'),
                    backgroundColor: AppTheme.trustBlue,
                  ),
                );
              },
              failure: (message) {
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  SnackBar(
                    content: Text(message),
                    backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
                  ),
                );
              },
              orElse: () {},
            );
          },
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppTheme.defaultPadding),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
                Text(
                  'Enter Verification Code',
                  style: context.textTheme.displaySmall,
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                RichText(
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  text: TextSpan(
                    style: context.textTheme.bodyLarge,
                    children: [
                      const TextSpan(text: 'We sent a 6-digit code to '),
                      TextSpan(
                        text: '+216 $phoneNumber',
                        style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing * 2),
                SmsOtpInput(phoneNumber: phoneNumber),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

class LoginForm extends StatefulWidget {
  const LoginForm({super.key});

  @override
  State<LoginForm> createState() => _LoginFormState();
}

class _LoginFormState extends State<LoginForm> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _phoneController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  bool _obscureText = true;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _phoneController.dispose();
    _passwordController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _onLoginPressed() {
    if (_formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
      context.read<AuthBloc>().add(
            AuthEvent.loginButtonPressed(
              phoneNumber: _phoneController.text.trim(),
              password: _passwordController.text.trim(),
            ),
          );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Form(
      key: _formKey,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          PrimaryTextField(
            label: 'Phone Number',
            hintText: 'e.g. 50123456',
            controller: _phoneController,
            keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
            prefixIcon: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16, horizontal: 12),
              child: Text(
                '+216',
                style: context.textTheme.bodyLarge?.copyWith(
                  color: AppTheme.darkGray,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            validator: validateTunisianPhoneNumber,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.defaultPadding),
          PrimaryTextField(
            label: 'Password',
            hintText: 'Enter your password',
            controller: _passwordController,
            obscureText: _obscureText,
            validator: validatePassword,
            suffixIcon: IconButton(
              icon: Icon(
                _obscureText ? Icons.visibility_off : Icons.visibility,
                color: AppTheme.disabledGray,
              ),
              onPressed: () {
                setState(() {
                  _obscureText = !_obscureText;
                });
              },
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
          BlocBuilder<AuthBloc, AuthState>(
            builder: (context, state) {
              final isLoading = state.maybeWhen(
                loading: () => true,
                orElse: () => false,
              );
              return PrimaryButton(
                text: 'Login',
                onPressed: _onLoginPressed,
                isLoading: isLoading,
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/utils/date_formatters.dart';

class ProfileSetupForm extends StatefulWidget {
  final Function({
    required String firstName,
    required String dateOfBirth,
    required String primaryGymId,
    required List<int> goalTagIds,
    required List<int> scheduleTagIds,
    String? profilePhotoUrl,
  }) onRegister;

  const ProfileSetupForm({super.key, required this.onRegister});

  @override
  State<ProfileSetupForm> createState() => _ProfileSetupFormState();
}

class _ProfileSetupFormState extends State<ProfileSetupForm> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _firstNameController = TextEditingController();
  final _dobController = TextEditingController();

  DateTime? _selectedDate;
  String? _selectedGymId;
  final List<int> _selectedGoalIds = [];
  final List<int> _selectedScheduleIds = [];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // Fetch data when the widget is initialized
    context.read<GymListCubit>().fetchApprovedGyms();
    context.read<TagsCubit>().loadAllTags();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _firstNameController.dispose();
    _dobController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _pickDate(BuildContext context) async {
    final DateTime? picked = await showDatePicker(
      context: context,
      initialDate: _selectedDate ?? DateTime(2000, 1, 1),
      firstDate: DateTime(1950),
      lastDate: DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 365 * 18)),
      helpText: 'Select Your Date of Birth',
    );
    if (picked != null && picked != _selectedDate) {
      setState(() {
        _selectedDate = picked;
        _dobController.text = formatToYyyyMmDd(picked); // Format for backend
      });
    }
  }

  void _onRegisterPressed() {
    if (_formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
      if (_selectedGymId == null) {
        _showError('Please select your primary gym.');
        return;
      }
      if (_selectedGoalIds.isEmpty) {
        _showError('Please select at least one goal.');
        return;
      }
      if (_selectedScheduleIds.isEmpty) {
        _showError('Please select your schedule.');
        return;
      }

      widget.onRegister(
        firstName: _firstNameController.text.trim(),
        dateOfBirth: _dobController.text,
        primaryGymId: _selectedGymId!,
        goalTagIds: _selectedGoalIds,
        scheduleTagIds: _selectedScheduleIds,
        // profilePhotoUrl: null, // TODO: Add profile photo upload logic
      );
    }
  }

  void _showError(String message) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Text(message),
        backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppTheme.defaultPadding),
      child: Form(
        key: _formKey,
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            Text(
              'Tell us about you',
              style: context.textTheme.displaySmall,
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
            PrimaryTextField(
              label: 'First Name',
              hintText: 'e.g. Ahmed',
              controller: _firstNameController,
              validator: (value) => validateNotEmpty(value, 'First Name'),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.defaultPadding),
            PrimaryTextField(
              label: 'Date of Birth',
              hintText: 'Select your birthday',
              controller: _dobController,
              readOnly: true,
              onTap: () => _pickDate(context),
              suffixIcon: const Icon(Icons.calendar_today),
              validator: (value) => validateNotEmpty(value, 'Date of Birth'),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.defaultPadding),
            _buildGymSelector(),
            const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
            _buildTagSelector(),
            const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
            BlocBuilder<AuthBloc, AuthState>(
              builder: (context, state) {
                final isLoading = state.maybeWhen(
                  loading: () => true,
                  orElse: () => false,
                );
                return PrimaryButton(
                  text: 'Create Account',
                  onPressed: _onRegisterPressed,
                  isLoading: isLoading,
                );
              },
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildGymSelector() {
    return BlocBuilder<GymListCubit, GymListState>(
      builder: (context, state) {
        return state.maybeWhen(
          loading: () => const LoadingIndicator(),
          error: (message) => Text('Error: $message'), // <-- CORRECTED
          loaded: (gyms) { // <-- CORRECTED
            return DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
              value: _selectedGymId,
              hint: const Text('Select your primary gym'),
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'Primary Gym',
              ),
              items: gyms.map((Gym gym) {
                return DropdownMenuItem<String>(
                  value: gym.gymId,
                  child: Text(gym.name),
                );
              }).toList(),
              onChanged: (String? newValue) {
                setState(() {
                  _selectedGymId = newValue;
                });
              },
              validator: (value) =>
                  value == null ? 'Please select a gym.' : null,
            );
          },
          orElse: () => const SizedBox.shrink(),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildTagSelector() {
    return BlocBuilder<TagsCubit, TagsState>(
      builder: (context, state) {
        return state.maybeWhen(
          loading: () => const LoadingIndicator(),
          error: (message) => Text('Error: $message'), // <-- CORRECTED
          loaded: (goalTags, scheduleTags) { // <-- CORRECTED
            return Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                _buildTagSection(
                  'Your Goals',
                  goalTags,
                  _selectedGoalIds,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.defaultPadding),
                _buildTagSection(
                  'Your Schedule',
                  scheduleTags,
                  _selectedScheduleIds,
                ),
              ],
            );
          },
          orElse: () => const SizedBox.shrink(),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildTagSection(String title, List<Tag> tags, List<int> selectedIds) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text(title, style: context.textTheme.titleMedium),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Wrap(
          spacing: 8.0,
          runSpacing: 8.0,
          children: tags.map((tag) {
            final isSelected = selectedIds.contains(tag.tagId);
            return TagChip(
              label: tag.tagName,
              selected: isSelected,
              onTap: () {
                setState(() {
                  if (isSelected) {
                    selectedIds.remove(tag.tagId);
                  } else {
                    selectedIds.add(tag.tagId);
                  }
                });
              },
            );
          }).toList(),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/services/recaptcha_service.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/utils/phone_number_validator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/utils/validators.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/buttons/custom_buttons.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/inputs/custom_inputs.dart';

class RegisterFormStep1 extends StatefulWidget {
  final Function(String phoneNumber, String password, String recaptchaToken)
      onNext;
  const RegisterFormStep1({super.key, required this.onNext});

  @override
  State<RegisterFormStep1> createState() => _RegisterFormStep1State();
}

class _RegisterFormStep1State extends State<RegisterFormStep1> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _phoneController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
  final _confirmPasswordController = TextEditingController();

  bool _obscurePassword = true;
  bool _obscureConfirmPassword = true;
  bool _isLoading = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _phoneController.dispose();
    _passwordController.dispose();
    _confirmPasswordController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _onNextPressed() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isLoading = true);

    try {
      // Get reCAPTCHA token
      final recaptchaToken =
          await getIt<ReCaptchaService>().getToken('register');

      // Pass data to parent page
      widget.onNext(
        _phoneController.text.trim(),
        _passwordController.text.trim(),
        recaptchaToken,
      );
    } catch (e) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text('reCAPTCHA failed: ${e.toString()}'),
          backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
        ),
      );
    } finally {
      setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppTheme.defaultPadding),
      child: Form(
        key: _formKey,
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            Text(
              'Your Account',
              style: context.textTheme.displaySmall,
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
            PrimaryTextField(
              label: 'Phone Number',
              hintText: 'e.g. 50123456',
              controller: _phoneController,
              keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
              prefixIcon: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16, horizontal: 12),
                child: Text(
                  '+216',
                  style: context.textTheme.bodyLarge?.copyWith(
                    color: AppTheme.darkGray,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              validator: validateTunisianPhoneNumber,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.defaultPadding),
            PrimaryTextField(
              label: 'Password',
              hintText: 'At least 8 characters',
              controller: _passwordController,
              obscureText: _obscurePassword,
              validator: validatePassword,
              suffixIcon: IconButton(
                icon: Icon(
                  _obscurePassword ? Icons.visibility_off : Icons.visibility,
                  color: AppTheme.disabledGray,
                ),
                onPressed: () {
                  setState(() {
                    _obscurePassword = !_obscurePassword;
                  });
                },
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.defaultPadding),
            PrimaryTextField(
              label: 'Confirm Password',
              hintText: 'Re-enter your password',
              controller: _confirmPasswordController,
              obscureText: _obscureConfirmPassword,
              validator: (value) {
                if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
                  return 'Please confirm your password.';
                }
                if (value != _passwordController.text) {
                  return 'Passwords do not match.';
                }
                return null;
              },
              suffixIcon: IconButton(
                icon: Icon(
                  _obscureConfirmPassword
                      ? Icons.visibility_off
                      : Icons.visibility,
                  color: AppTheme.disabledGray,
                ),
                onPressed: () {
                  setState(() {
                    _obscureConfirmPassword = !_obscureConfirmPassword;
                  });
                },
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
            PrimaryButton(
              text: 'Next',
              onPressed: _onNextPressed,
              isLoading: _isLoading,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
