import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/services/recaptcha_service.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/utils/validators.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/auth/presentation/bloc/auth_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/buttons/custom_buttons.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/inputs/custom_inputs.dart';

class SmsOtpInput extends StatefulWidget {
  final String phoneNumber;
  const SmsOtpInput({super.key, required this.phoneNumber});

  @override
  State<SmsOtpInput> createState() => _SmsOtpInputState();
}

class _SmsOtpInputState extends State<SmsOtpInput> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _otpController = TextEditingController();

  Timer? _resendTimer;
  int _countdown = 60;
  bool _isResending = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _startResendTimer();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _resendTimer?.cancel();
    _otpController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _startResendTimer() {
    setState(() => _countdown = 60);
    _resendTimer?.cancel(); // Cancel any existing timer
    _resendTimer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (timer) {
      if (_countdown > 0) {
        setState(() => _countdown--);
      } else {
        timer.cancel();
      }
    });
  }

  Future<void> _onResendPressed() async {
    if (_countdown > 0) return;

    setState(() => _isResending = true);
    try {
      final recaptchaToken =
          await getIt<ReCaptchaService>().getToken('resend-otp');
      if (mounted) {
        context.read<AuthBloc>().add(
              AuthEvent.resendOtpButtonPressed(
                phoneNumber: widget.phoneNumber,
                recaptchaToken: recaptchaToken,
              ),
            );
      }
      _startResendTimer();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text('reCAPTCHA failed: ${e.toString()}'),
            backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
          ),
        );
      }
    } finally {
      setState(() => _isResending = false);
    }
  }

  void _onVerifyPressed() {
    if (_formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
      context.read<AuthBloc>().add(
            AuthEvent.verifySmsButtonPressed(
              phoneNumber: widget.phoneNumber,
              otp: _otpController.text.trim(),
            ),
          );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Form(
      key: _formKey,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          PrimaryTextField(
            label: '6-Digit OTP',
            hintText: '------',
            controller: _otpController,
            keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
            validator: validateOtp,
            maxLength: 6,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
          BlocBuilder<AuthBloc, AuthState>(
            builder: (context, state) {
              final isLoading = state.maybeWhen(
                loading: () => true,
                orElse: () => false,
              );
              return PrimaryButton(
                text: 'Verify',
                onPressed: _onVerifyPressed,
                isLoading: isLoading,
              );
            },
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.defaultPadding),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: _countdown == 0 ? _onResendPressed : null,
            child: _isResending
                ? const SizedBox(
                    height: 16,
                    width: 16,
                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),
                  )
                : Text(
                    _countdown == 0
                        ? 'Resend OTP'
                        : 'Resend OTP in $_countdown s',
                  ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\Ahmed\Desktop\sahbifit\mobile\lib\features\chat\data\datasources\chat_remote_datasource.dart
==================================================
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/data/models/message_model.dart';

abstract class ChatRemoteDatasource {
  Stream<List<MessageModel>> getChatStream(String sessionId);
  Future<void> sendMessage(String sessionId, MessageModel message);
}

@Injectable(as: ChatRemoteDatasource)
class ChatRemoteDatasourceImpl implements ChatRemoteDatasource {
  final FirebaseFirestore _firestore;

  ChatRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._firestore);

  // Your backend's FirebaseChatService.java creates docs in "session_chats"
  static const String _collection = 'session_chats';
  static const String _subCollection = 'messages';

  @override
  Stream<List<MessageModel>> getChatStream(String sessionId) {
    // 1. Get the collection of messages for the given session
    final collectionRef = _firestore
        .collection(_collection)
        .doc(sessionId)
        .collection(_subCollection)
        .orderBy('timestamp', descending: true); // Order by time

    // 2. Get a stream of snapshots
    return collectionRef.snapshots().map((querySnapshot) {
      // 3. For each snapshot, map the documents into MessageModels
      return querySnapshot.docs
          .map((doc) => MessageModel.fromSnapshot(doc))
          .toList();
    });
  }

  @override
  Future<void> sendMessage(String sessionId, MessageModel message) async {
    // 1. Get the reference to the "messages" subcollection
    final collectionRef = _firestore
        .collection(_collection)
        .doc(sessionId)
        .collection(_subCollection);

    // 2. Add the new message
    // We use .toFirestore() to convert the model to a map
    // and add the server-side timestamp.
    await collectionRef.add(message.toFirestore());
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\Ahmed\Desktop\sahbifit\mobile\lib\features\chat\data\models\message_model.dart

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/domain/entities/message.dart'; // Ensure this path is correct

part 'message_model.freezed.dart';
part 'message_model.g.dart';

// [DELETED] @JsonSerializable annotation removed from here

@freezed
abstract class MessageModel with _$MessageModel {
  const MessageModel._();
  
  const factory MessageModel({
    String? id, // [FIXED]
    required String senderId,
    required String text,
    // When reading, 'timestamp' will be a Timestamp.
    // When writing, this will be null and replaced by toFirestore
    required DateTime? timestamp,
  }) = _MessageModel;

  /// Special factory for creating a model to *send* to Firestore
  /// This sets the timestamp to null, so we can use a server timestamp.
  factory MessageModel.forSending({
    required String senderId,
    required String text,
  }) =>
      MessageModel(
        senderId: senderId,
        text: text,
        timestamp: null, // Will be replaced by server timestamp
      );

  /// Factory for creating a model from a Firestore snapshot
  factory MessageModel.fromSnapshot(DocumentSnapshot doc) {
    final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>;
    // Use the generated fromJson factory, then add the doc ID
    return MessageModel.fromJson(data).copyWith(id: doc.id);
  }

  /// Factory for creating a model from a JSON map (required by freezed)
  @JsonSerializable( // [MOVED] Annotation is now here
    fieldRename: FieldRename.snake,
    converters: [TimestampConverter()],
    explicitToJson: true,
  )
  factory MessageModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$MessageModelFromJson(json);

  /// Converts this model into a Map to *write* to Firestore
  Map<String, dynamic> toFirestore() {
    // toJson() will now exist thanks to the .fromJson factory being added
    // and build_runner being run.
    final json = toJson();

    // Replace our null timestamp with the server's command
    json['timestamp'] = FieldValue.serverTimestamp();

    // Don't write the 'id' field back into the document data
    json.remove('id');

    return json;
  }

  // Converts the data-layer model to a domain-layer entity
  Message toEntity() {
    return Message(
      messageId: id ?? '', // Use the ID from the snapshot
      senderId: senderId,
      text: text,
      timestamp: timestamp ?? DateTime.now(), // Fallback if timestamp is null
    );
  }
}

// This is the custom converter for handling Firestore Timestamps
// [FIXED] Changed Object to Object? to handle null values correctly.
class TimestampConverter implements JsonConverter<DateTime?, Object?> {
  const TimestampConverter();

  @override
  DateTime? fromJson(Object? json) { // [FIXED] Changed Object to Object?
    if (json is Timestamp) {
      return json.toDate();
    }
    // Handle cases where it might already be null
    if (json == null) {
      return null;
    }
    // You could add more robust parsing here if needed, e.g., from String
    return null;
  }

  @override
  Object toJson(DateTime? time) {
    // If a time is provided, convert it to a Timestamp
    if (time != null) {
      return Timestamp.fromDate(time);
    }
    // If time is null, we intend to use a server timestamp.
    // The `toFirestore` method handles this explicitly, but setting
    // it here ensures consistency if toJson() were called directly.
    return FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
  }
}

_MessageModel _$MessageModelFromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
    _MessageModel(
      id: json['id'] as String?,
      senderId: json['senderId'] as String,
      text: json['text'] as String,
      timestamp: json['timestamp'] == null
          ? null
          : DateTime.parse(json['timestamp'] as String),

    <String, dynamic>{
      'id': instance.id,
      'senderId': instance.senderId,
      'text': instance.text,
      'timestamp': instance.timestamp?.toIso8601String(),
    };

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/data/datasources/chat_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/data/models/message_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/domain/entities/message.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/domain/repositories/chat_repository.dart';

@Injectable(as: ChatRepository)
class ChatRepositoryImpl implements ChatRepository {
  final ChatRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;

  ChatRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource);

  @override
  Stream<List<Message>> getChatStream(String sessionId) {
    // We catch errors on the stream itself
    return _remoteDatasource.getChatStream(sessionId).map((models) {
      return models.map((model) => model.toEntity()).toList();
    }).handleError((error) {
      // Log the error and bubble up a generic failure
      print('Firestore Stream Error: $error');
      throw ServerFailure(message: 'Could not load chat messages.');
    });
  }

  @override
  Future<void> sendMessage({
    required String sessionId,
    required String senderId,
    required String text,
  }) async {
    try {
      // Create the model to send
      final message = MessageModel.forSending(
        senderId: senderId,
        text: text,
      );
      await _remoteDatasource.sendMessage(sessionId, message);
    } on FirebaseException catch (e) {
      // Handle specific Firestore errors
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message ?? 'Failed to send message.');
    } catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: 'An unknown error occurred.');
    }
  }
}

import 'dart:async'; // Required for StreamSubscription

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/domain/entities/message.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/domain/usecases/get_chat_stream.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/domain/usecases/send_message.dart';

part 'chat_cubit.freezed.dart';
part 'chat_state.dart';

@injectable
class ChatCubit extends Cubit<ChatState> {
  final GetChatStream _getChatStream;
  final SendMessage _sendMessage;

  // This subscription needs to be stored so we can cancel it later.
  StreamSubscription<List<Message>>? _messageSubscription;

  ChatCubit(
    this._getChatStream,
    this._sendMessage,
  ) : super(const ChatState.initial());

  /// Connects to the real-time message stream for a specific session.
  Future<void> listenToChat(String sessionId) async {
    // Cancel any previous subscription before starting a new one
    await _messageSubscription?.cancel();
    emit(const ChatState.loading());

    try {
      // 1. Call the use case to get the stream
      final messageStream = await _getChatStream(
        ChatStreamParams(sessionId: sessionId),
      );

      // 2. Listen to the stream
      _messageSubscription = messageStream.listen(
        (messages) {
          // 3. Every time new data comes in, emit a 'loaded' state
          emit(ChatState.loaded(messages: messages));
        },
        onError: (error) {
          // 4. Handle any errors that occur on the stream
          emit(ChatState.error(error.toString()));
        },
      );
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(ChatState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ChatState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  /// Sends a new chat message.
  Future<void> sendMessage({
    required String sessionId,
    required String senderId, // This should be the ID of the logged-in user
    required String text,
  }) async {
    // Don't emit a loading state here.
    // The UI will update optimistically when the stream listener
    // receives the new message from Firestore.
    try {
      final params = SendMessageParams(
        sessionId: sessionId,
        senderId: senderId,
        text: text,
      );
      await _sendMessage(params);
      // On success, do nothing. The stream listener will update the state.
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      // If sending fails, emit an error state
      emit(ChatState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ChatState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  /// Overrides the [close] method to cancel the stream subscription
  /// when the Cubit is disposed. This is CRITICAL to prevent memory leaks.
  @override
  Future<void> close() {
    _messageSubscription?.cancel();
    return super.close();
  }
}

/// A `switch`-like method, using callbacks.
///
/// As opposed to `map`, this offers destructuring.
/// It is equivalent to doing:
/// ```dart
/// switch (sealedClass) {
///   case Subclass(:final field):
///     return ...;
///   case Subclass2(:final field2):
///     return ...;
/// }
/// ```

@optionalTypeArgs TResult when<TResult extends Object?>({required TResult Function()  initial,required TResult Function()  loading,required TResult Function( List<Message> messages)  loaded,required TResult Function( String message)  error,}) {final _that = this;
switch (_that) {
case _Initial():
return initial();case _Loading():
return loading();case _Loaded():
return loaded(_that.messages);case _Error():
return error(_that.message);}
}
/// A variant of `when` that fallback to returning `null`
///
/// It is equivalent to doing:
/// ```dart
/// switch (sealedClass) {
///   case Subclass(:final field):
///     return ...;
///   case _:
///     return null;
/// }
/// ```

@optionalTypeArgs TResult? whenOrNull<TResult extends Object?>({TResult? Function()?  initial,TResult? Function()?  loading,TResult? Function( List<Message> messages)?  loaded,TResult? Function( String message)?  error,}) {final _that = this;
switch (_that) {
case _Initial() when initial != null:
return initial();case _Loading() when loading != null:
return loading();case _Loaded() when loaded != null:
return loaded(_that.messages);case _Error() when error != null:
return error(_that.message);case _:
  return null;

}
}

}

part of 'chat_cubit.dart';

@freezed
sealed class ChatState with _$ChatState {
  /// Chat has not been initialized or connected.
  const factory ChatState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// Connecting to the chat stream.
  const factory ChatState.loading() = _Loading;

  /// Successfully connected and listening for messages.
  const factory ChatState.loaded({required List<Message> messages}) = _Loaded;

  /// An error occurred (either connecting or sending a message).
  const factory ChatState.error(String message) = _Error;
}

import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/app/bloc/app_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/domain/entities/message.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/presentation/cubit/chat_cubit.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/presentation/widgets/chat_disabled_placeholder.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/presentation/widgets/chat_input_field.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/presentation/widgets/chat_logistics_header.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/presentation/widgets/message_bubble.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/app_bars/custom_app_bar.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/states/loading_states.dart';

@RoutePage()
class ChatPage extends StatelessWidget {
  final String sessionId;

  const ChatPage({
    super.key,
    @PathParam('sessionId') required this.sessionId,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // Provides the Cubit to this page and its children.
    // It also triggers the initial data load.
    return BlocProvider(
      create: (context) => getIt<ChatCubit>()..listenToChat(sessionId),
      child: BlocBuilder<AppBloc, AppState>(
        // We need the AppBloc to get the current user's ID
        builder: (context, appState) {
          // Assume AppState has an 'authenticated' state with a 'user' object
          final currentUserId =
              appState.mapOrNull(authenticated: (state) => state.user.userId);

          if (currentUserId == null) {
            // This case should ideally not happen if routing is correct,
            // but it's good practice to handle it.
            return const Scaffold(
              body: LoadingIndicator(message: 'Authenticating...'),
            );
          }

          return Scaffold(
            appBar: const CustomAppBar(title: 'Session Chat'),
            body: Column(
              children: [
                const ChatLogisticsHeader(),
                Expanded(
                  // This builder handles the chat's own state
                  child: BlocBuilder<ChatCubit, ChatState>(
                    builder: (context, chatState) {
                      return chatState.when(
                        initial: () => const SizedBox.shrink(),
                        loading: () =>
                            const LoadingIndicator(message: 'Loading chat...'),
                        error: (message) => ErrorStateWidget(
                          message: message,
                          buttonText: 'Retry',
                          onRetry: () {
                            context.read<ChatCubit>().listenToChat(sessionId);
                          },
                        ),
                        loaded: (messages) => _buildChatList(
                          context,
                          messages,
                          currentUserId,
                        ),
                      );
                    },
                  ),
                ),
                ChatInputField(
                  enabled: true, // Add logic here if chat can be disabled
                  onSend: (text) {
                    context.read<ChatCubit>().sendMessage(
                          sessionId: sessionId,
                          senderId: currentUserId,
                          text: text,
                        );
                  },
                ),
              ],
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildChatList(
    BuildContext context,
    List<Message> messages,
    String currentUserId,
  ) {
    if (messages.isEmpty) {
      return const ChatDisabledPlaceholder(
        title: 'Chat is Empty',
        message: 'Be the first to send a message!',
        icon: Icons.chat_rounded,
      );
    }

    return ListView.builder(
      reverse: true, // This makes the chat feel natural
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
      itemCount: messages.length,
      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
        final message = messages[index];
        return MessageBubble(
          message: message,
          isMe: message.senderId == currentUserId,
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';

class ChatInputField extends StatefulWidget {
  final bool enabled;
  final Function(String text) onSend;

  const ChatInputField({
    super.key,
    this.enabled = true,
    required this.onSend,
  });

  @override
  State<ChatInputField> createState() => _ChatInputFieldState();
}

class _ChatInputFieldState extends State<ChatInputField> {
  final _controller = TextEditingController();
  bool _canSend = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _controller.addListener(() {
      final canSend = _controller.text.trim().isNotEmpty;
      if (canSend != _canSend) {
        setState(() {
          _canSend = canSend;
        });
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _handleSend() {
    if (_canSend && widget.enabled) {
      widget.onSend(_controller.text.trim());
      _controller.clear();
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final bool isEffectivelyEnabled = widget.enabled && _canSend;

    return Container(
      padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
        16,
        16,
        16,
        16 + MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom,
      ),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppTheme.cleanWhite,
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: AppTheme.darkGray.withOpacity(0.1),
            blurRadius: 8,
            offset: const Offset(0, -2),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: TextField(
              controller: _controller,
              enabled: widget.enabled,
              textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.sentences,
              autocorrect: true,
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                hintText: widget.enabled
                    ? 'Type a message...'
                    : 'Chat is not available',
                filled: true,
                fillColor: AppTheme.lightGray,
                border: theme.inputDecorationTheme.border,
                enabledBorder: theme.inputDecorationTheme.enabledBorder,
                focusedBorder: theme.inputDecorationTheme.focusedBorder,
                contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
              ),
              onSubmitted: (_) => _handleSend(),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.send_rounded),
            iconSize: 24,
            color: isEffectivelyEnabled
                ? AppTheme.primaryRed
                : AppTheme.disabledGray,
            onPressed: isEffectivelyEnabled ? _handleSend : null,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/utils/date_formatters.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/chat/domain/entities/message.dart';

class MessageBubble extends StatelessWidget {
  final Message message;
  final bool isMe;

  const MessageBubble({
    super.key,
    required this.message,
    required this.isMe,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final alignment = isMe ? CrossAxisAlignment.end : CrossAxisAlignment.start;
    final color = isMe ? AppTheme.primaryRed : AppTheme.lightGray;
    final textColor = isMe ? AppTheme.cleanWhite : AppTheme.darkGray;

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4, horizontal: 8),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: alignment,
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: color,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(
                topLeft: const Radius.circular(16),
                topRight: const Radius.circular(16),
                bottomLeft: isMe ? const Radius.circular(16) : Radius.zero,
                bottomRight: isMe ? Radius.zero : const Radius.circular(16),
              ),
            ),
            constraints: BoxConstraints(
              maxWidth: MediaQuery.of(context).size.width * 0.75,
            ),
            child: Text(
              message.text,
              style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(color: textColor),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Text(
            formatReadableTime(message.timestamp.toIso8601String()),
            style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
              color: AppTheme.disabledGray,
              fontSize: 10,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/api/api_client.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/constants/api_endpoints.dart'; // <-- IMPORT
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/data/models/gym_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/data/models/gym_submission_model.dart';

abstract class GymRemoteDatasource {
  /// Calls the GET /gyms endpoint
  Future<List<GymModel>> getApprovedGyms();

  /// Calls the POST /gyms/submit endpoint
  Future<void> submitGym(GymSubmissionModel request);
}

@Injectable(as: GymRemoteDatasource)
class GymRemoteDatasourceImpl implements GymRemoteDatasource {
  final ApiClient _apiClient;

  GymRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._apiClient);

  @override
  Future<List<GymModel>> getApprovedGyms() async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.get(ApiEndpoints.gyms); // <-- USE CONSTANT

      // The response.data is a List<dynamic> of JSON maps
      // We must convert it into a List<GymModel>
      return (response.data as List)
          .map((json) => GymModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> submitGym(GymSubmissionModel request) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.submitGym, // <-- USE CONSTANT
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }
}

@freezed
abstract class GymModel with _$GymModel {
  const GymModel._(); // Private constructor for methods

  
  const factory GymModel({
    required String gymId,
    required String name,
    String? address,
    required GymStatus status,
    required int memberCount,
  }) = _GymModel;

  factory GymModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$GymModelFromJson(json);

  // Converts the data-layer model to a domain-layer entity
  Gym toEntity() {
    return Gym(
      gymId: gymId,
      name: name,
      address: address,
      status: status,
      memberCount: memberCount,
    );
  }
}

import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/data/datasources/gym_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/data/models/gym_submission_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/entities/gym.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/entities/gym_submission.dart'; 
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/repositories/gym_repository.dart';

@Injectable(as: GymRepository)
class GymRepositoryImpl implements GymRepository {
  final GymRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;

  GymRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource);

  @override
  Future<List<Gym>> getApprovedGyms() async {
    try {
      // 
      // ðŸ’¡ THIS IS THE CORRECTED LINE:
      //
      final gymModels = await _remoteDatasource.getApprovedGyms();
      //
      //
      return gymModels.map((model) => model.toEntity()).toList();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> submitGym(GymSubmission submission) async {
    try {
      final requestModel = GymSubmissionModel(
        name: submission.name,
        address: submission.address,
      );
      await _remoteDatasource.submitGym(requestModel);
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/entities/gym.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/usecases/get_approved_gyms.dart';

part 'gym_list_cubit.freezed.dart';
part 'gym_list_state.dart';

@injectable
class GymListCubit extends Cubit<GymListState> {
  final GetApprovedGyms _getApprovedGyms;

  GymListCubit(this._getApprovedGyms) : super(const GymListState.initial());

  /// Fetches the list of all approved gyms.
  Future<void> fetchApprovedGyms() async {
    emit(const GymListState.loading());
    try {
      final gyms = await _getApprovedGyms(NoParams());
      emit(GymListState.loaded(gyms: gyms));
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(GymListState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(GymListState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

@freezed
sealed class GymListState with _$GymListState {
  /// The initial state, before gyms are fetched.
  const factory GymListState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// The state while fetching the list of gyms.
  const factory GymListState.loading() = _Loading;

  const factory GymListState.loaded({required List<Gym> gyms}) = _Loaded;

  /// The state when an error occurs.
  const factory GymListState.error(String message) = _Error;
}

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/entities/gym_submission.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/usecases/submit_gym.dart';

part 'submit_gym_cubit.freezed.dart';
part 'submit_gym_state.dart';

@injectable
class SubmitGymCubit extends Cubit<SubmitGymState> {
  final SubmitGym _submitGym;

  SubmitGymCubit(this._submitGym) : super(const SubmitGymState.initial());

  /// Submits a new gym for approval.
  Future<void> submitGym(String name, String address) async {
    emit(const SubmitGymState.loading());
    try {
      final submission = GymSubmission(name: name, address: address);
      await _submitGym(submission);
      emit(const SubmitGymState.success());
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(SubmitGymState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(SubmitGymState.error('An unexpected error occurred: $e'));
    }
  }
}

@freezed
sealed class SubmitGymState with _$SubmitGymState {
  /// The initial state, before any submission.

import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/presentation/cubit/gym_list_cubit.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/app_bars/custom_app_bar.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/states/loading_states.dart';

@RoutePage()
class GymListPage extends StatelessWidget {
  const GymListPage({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocProvider(
      create: (context) => getIt<GymListCubit>()..fetchApprovedGyms(),
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: const CustomAppBar(title: 'Approved Gyms'),
        body: BlocBuilder<GymListCubit, GymListState>(
          builder: (context, state) {
            return state.when(
              initial: () =>
                  const LoadingIndicator(message: 'Loading gyms...'),
              loading: () =>
                  const LoadingIndicator(message: 'Loading gyms...'),
              error: (message) => ErrorStateWidget(
                message: message,
                buttonText: 'Retry',
                onRetry: () {
                  context.read<GymListCubit>().fetchApprovedGyms();
                },
              ),
              loaded: (gyms) {
                if (gyms.isEmpty) {
                  return const Center(
                    child: EmptyStateWidget(
                      icon: Icons.search_off_rounded,
                      title: 'No Gyms Found',
                      message:
                          'There are no approved gyms in the system yet. You can submit one!',
                    ),
                  );
                }

                return ListView.separated(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppTheme.defaultPadding),
                  itemCount: gyms.length,
                  separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const Divider(),
                  itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                    final gym = gyms[index];
                    return ListTile(
                      title: Text(gym.name,
                          style: context.textTheme.titleMedium),
                      subtitle: Text(
                        gym.address ?? 'No address provided',
                        style: context.textTheme.bodyMedium
                            ?.copyWith(color: AppTheme.disabledGray),
                      ),
                      trailing: Column(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            gym.memberCount.toString(),
                            style: context.textTheme.titleMedium
                                ?.copyWith(color: AppTheme.primaryRed),
                          ),
                          Text(
                            'members',
                            style: context.textTheme.bodySmall
                                ?.copyWith(color: AppTheme.disabledGray),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    );
                  },
                );
              },
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/utils/validators.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/presentation/cubit/submit_gym_cubit.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/app_bars/custom_app_bar.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/buttons/custom_buttons.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/inputs/custom_inputs.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/states/loading_states.dart';

@RoutePage()
class SubmitGymPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const SubmitGymPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<SubmitGymPage> createState() => _SubmitGymPageState();
}

class _SubmitGymPageState extends State<SubmitGymPage> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _nameController = TextEditingController();
  final _addressController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _addressController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _onSubmit(BuildContext context) {
    if (_formKey.currentState?.validate() ?? false) {
      context.read<SubmitGymCubit>().submitGym(
            _nameController.text,
            _addressController.text,
          );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocProvider(
      create: (context) => getIt<SubmitGymCubit>(),
      child: BlocConsumer<SubmitGymCubit, SubmitGymState>(
        listener: (context, state) {
          state.maybeWhen(
            success: () {
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                const SnackBar(
                  content: Text('Gym submitted successfully for review!'),
                  backgroundColor: AppTheme.successGreen,
                ),
              );
              context.router.pop();
            },
            error: (message) {
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(
                  content: Text(message),
                  backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
                ),
              );
            },
            orElse: () {},
          );
        },
        builder: (context, state) {
          final isLoading = state.maybeWhen(
            loading: () => true,
            orElse: () => false,
          );

          return Scaffold(
            appBar: const CustomAppBar(title: 'Submit a New Gym'),
            body: Stack(
              children: [
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppTheme.defaultPadding),
                  child: Form(
                    key: _formKey,
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          'Don\'t see your gym? Add it to our list. It will be reviewed by our team.',
                          style: context.textTheme.bodyMedium,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
                        PrimaryTextField(
                          label: 'Gym Name',
                          hintText: 'e.g., "California Gym"',
                          controller: _nameController,
                          validator: (val) => validateNotEmpty(val, 'Gym Name'),
                          enabled: !isLoading,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.defaultPadding),
                        PrimaryTextField(
                          label: 'Address',
                          hintText: 'e.g., "Lac 1, Tunis"',
                          controller: _addressController,
                          validator: (val) => validateNotEmpty(val, 'Address'),
                          enabled: !isLoading,
                        ),
                        const Spacer(),
                        PrimaryButton(
                          text: 'Submit for Review',
                          onPressed: () => _onSubmit(context),
                          isLoading: isLoading,
                          enabled: !isLoading,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.defaultPadding),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                if (isLoading)
                  const Center(child: LoadingIndicator(message: 'Submitting...')),
              ],
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/entities/gym.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/presentation/cubit/gym_list_cubit.dart';

/// A dropdown widget that fetches and displays a list of approved gyms.
class GymPicker extends StatefulWidget {
  final Function(Gym gym) onGymSelected;
  final String? errorText;
  final Gym? initialValue;
  final String label;

  const GymPicker({
    super.key,
    required this.onGymSelected,
    this.errorText,
    this.initialValue,
    this.label = 'Select Gym',
  });

  @override
  State<GymPicker> createState() => _GymPickerState();
}

class _GymPickerState extends State<GymPicker> {
  Gym? _selectedValue;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _selectedValue = widget.initialValue;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocProvider(
      create: (context) => getIt<GymListCubit>()..fetchApprovedGyms(),
      child: BlocBuilder<GymListCubit, GymListState>(
        builder: (context, state) {
          return state.when(
            initial: () => _buildDropdown(context, isLoading: true),
            loading: () => _buildDropdown(context, isLoading: true),
            error: (message) => _buildDropdown(context, error: message),
            loaded: (gyms) => _buildDropdown(
              context,
              gyms: gyms,
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDropdown(
    BuildContext context, {
    bool isLoading = false,
    String? error,
    List<Gym>? gyms,
  }) {
    // Ensure the selected value is still valid if the list reloads
    if (_selectedValue != null &&
        gyms != null &&
        !gyms.any((g) => g.gymId == _selectedValue!.gymId)) {
      _selectedValue = null;
    }

    return DropdownButtonFormField<Gym>(
      value: _selectedValue,
      items: gyms
          ?.map((gym) => DropdownMenuItem<Gym>(
                value: gym,
                child: Text(gym.name),
              ))
          .toList(),
      onChanged: isLoading
          ? null
          : (Gym? newValue) {
              if (newValue != null) {
                setState(() {
                  _selectedValue = newValue;
                });
                widget.onGymSelected(newValue);
              }
            },
      decoration: InputDecoration(
        labelText: widget.label,
        errorText: error ?? widget.errorText,
        prefixIcon: isLoading
            ? const SizedBox(
                width: 20,
                height: 20,
                child: Padding(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.all(12.0),
                  child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),
                ),
              )
            : const Icon(Icons.fitness_center_outlined),
      ),
      hint: Text(isLoading ? 'Loading gyms...' : 'Choose a gym'),
      disabledHint: Text(isLoading ? 'Loading gyms...' : 'No gyms available'),
      validator: (value) => value == null ? 'Please select a gym.' : null,
      autovalidateMode: AutovalidateMode.onUserInteraction,
    );
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\Ahmed\Desktop\sahbifit\mobile\lib\features\rating\data\datasources\rating_remote_datasource.dart

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/api/api_client.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/constants/api_endpoints.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/data/models/create_rating_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/data/models/pending_rating_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/data/models/rating_model.dart';

abstract class RatingRemoteDatasource {
  Future<List<PendingRatingModel>> getPendingRatings();
  Future<RatingModel> createRating(CreateRatingModel request);
  Future<List<RatingModel>> getRatingsForUser(String userId);
}

@Injectable(as: RatingRemoteDatasource)
class RatingRemoteDatasourceImpl implements RatingRemoteDatasource {
  final ApiClient _apiClient;

  RatingRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._apiClient);

  @override
  Future<List<PendingRatingModel>> getPendingRatings() async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.get(ApiEndpoints.pendingRatings);
      return (response.data as List)
          .map((json) => PendingRatingModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<RatingModel> createRating(CreateRatingModel request) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.ratings,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
      // Backend returns the full Rating object upon creation
      return RatingModel.fromJson(response.data);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<List<RatingModel>> getRatingsForUser(String userId) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.get(
        ApiEndpoints.ratingsForUser(userId),
      );
      return (response.data as List)
          .map((json) => RatingModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/entities/rating.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/data/models/user_model.dart';

part 'rating_model.freezed.dart';
part 'rating_model.g.dart';

// Helper model to represent the nested 'session' object
// We only extract the ID, as seen in the Rating entity

@freezed
abstract class RatingSessionModel with _$RatingSessionModel {
  
  const factory RatingSessionModel({
    required String sessionId,
  }) = _RatingSessionModel;

  factory RatingSessionModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$RatingSessionModelFromJson(json);
}



@freezed
abstract class RatingModel with _$RatingModel {
  const RatingModel._();

  
  const factory RatingModel({
    required String ratingId,
    required RatingSessionModel session, // Nested session object
    required UserModel ratingBy,
    required UserModel ratingFor,
    required int ratingValue,
    String? comment,
    required String createdAt, // Receive as ISO 8601 String
  }) = _RatingModel;

  factory RatingModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$RatingModelFromJson(json);

  Rating toEntity() {
    return Rating(
      ratingId: ratingId,
      sessionId: session.sessionId, // Extract the ID
      ratingBy: ratingBy.toEntity(),
      ratingFor: ratingFor.toEntity(),
      ratingValue: ratingValue,
      comment: comment,
      createdAt: DateTime.parse(createdAt), // Convert String to DateTime
    );
  }
}

import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/data/datasources/rating_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/data/models/create_rating_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/entities/pending_rating.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/entities/rating.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/repositories/rating_repository.dart';

@Injectable(as: RatingRepository)
class RatingRepositoryImpl implements RatingRepository {
  final RatingRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;

  RatingRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource);

  @override
  Future<List<PendingRating>> getPendingRatings() async {
    try {
      final models = await _remoteDatasource.getPendingRatings();
      return models.map((model) => model.toEntity()).toList();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
  
  @override
  Future<Rating> createRating(CreateRatingModel request) async {
    try {
      final model = await _remoteDatasource.createRating(request);
      return model.toEntity();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<List<Rating>> getRatingsForUser(String userId) async {
     try {
      final models = await _remoteDatasource.getRatingsForUser(userId);
      return models.map((model) => model.toEntity()).toList();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/data/models/create_rating_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/entities/rating.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/repositories/rating_repository.dart';

@injectable
class CreateRating implements UseCase<Rating, CreateRatingParams> {
  final RatingRepository _repository;

  CreateRating(this._repository);

  @override
  Future<Rating> call(CreateRatingParams params) async {
    // Map from clean params to the data model
    final requestModel = CreateRatingModel(
      sessionId: params.sessionId,
      ratedUserId: params.ratedUserId,
      ratingValue: params.ratingValue,
      comment: params.comment,
    );
    return _repository.createRating(requestModel);
  }
}

class CreateRatingParams extends Equatable {
  final String sessionId;
  final String ratedUserId;
  final int ratingValue;
  final String? comment;

  const CreateRatingParams({
    required this.sessionId,
    required this.ratedUserId,
    required this.ratingValue,
    this.comment,
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [sessionId, ratedUserId, ratingValue, comment];
}

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/entities/pending_rating.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/usecases/create_rating.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/usecases/get_pending_ratings.dart';

part 'pending_rating_bloc.freezed.dart';
part 'pending_rating_event.dart';
part 'pending_rating_state.dart';

@injectable
class PendingRatingBloc extends Bloc<PendingRatingEvent, PendingRatingState> {
  final GetPendingRatings _getPendingRatings;
  final CreateRating _createRating;

  PendingRatingBloc(
    this._getPendingRatings,
    this._createRating,
  ) : super(const PendingRatingState.initial()) {
    on<_FetchList>(_onFetchList);
    on<_SubmitRating>(_onSubmitRating);
  }

  Future<void> _onFetchList(
    _FetchList event,
    Emitter<PendingRatingState> emit,
  ) async {
    emit(const PendingRatingState.loading());
    try {
      final ratings = await _getPendingRatings(NoParams());
      emit(PendingRatingState.loaded(pendingRatings: ratings));
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(PendingRatingState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(PendingRatingState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _onSubmitRating(
    _SubmitRating event,
    Emitter<PendingRatingState> emit,
  ) async {
    // Keep the old state's list while loading
    // Or just emit a generic loading state
    emit(const PendingRatingState.loading());

    try {
      final params = CreateRatingParams(
        sessionId: event.sessionId,
        ratedUserId: event.ratedUserId,
        ratingValue: event.ratingValue,
        comment: event.comment,
      );
      await _createRating(params);

      // Emit success and then immediately refresh the list
      emit(const PendingRatingState.submissionSuccess());
      add(const PendingRatingEvent.fetchList());
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(PendingRatingState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(PendingRatingState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

///   case Subclass(:final field):
///     return ...;
///   case _:
///     return orElse();
/// }
/// ```

@optionalTypeArgs TResult maybeWhen<TResult extends Object?>({TResult Function()?  initial,TResult Function()?  loading,TResult Function( List<PendingRating> pendingRatings)?  loaded,TResult Function()?  submissionSuccess,TResult Function( String message)?  error,required TResult orElse(),}) {final _that = this;
switch (_that) {
case _Initial() when initial != null:
return initial();case _Loading() when loading != null:
return loading();case _Loaded() when loaded != null:
return loaded(_that.pendingRatings);case _SubmissionSuccess() when submissionSuccess != null:
return submissionSuccess();case _Error() when error != null:
return error(_that.message);case _:
  return orElse();

}
}
/// A `switch`-like method, using callbacks.
///
/// As opposed to `map`, this offers destructuring.
/// It is equivalent to doing:
/// ```dart
/// switch (sealedClass) {
///   case Subclass(:final field):
///     return ...;
///   case Subclass2(:final field2):
///     return ...;
/// }
/// ```

@optionalTypeArgs TResult when<TResult extends Object?>({required TResult Function()  initial,required TResult Function()  loading,required TResult Function( List<PendingRating> pendingRatings)  loaded,required TResult Function()  submissionSuccess,required TResult Function( String message)  error,}) {final _that = this;
switch (_that) {
case _Initial():
return initial();case _Loading():
return loading();case _Loaded():
return loaded(_that.pendingRatings);case _SubmissionSuccess():
return submissionSuccess();case _Error():
return error(_that.message);}
}
/// A variant of `when` that fallback to returning `null`
///
/// It is equivalent to doing:
/// ```dart
/// switch (sealedClass) {
///   case Subclass(:final field):
///     return ...;
///   case _:
///     return null;
/// }
/// ```

@optionalTypeArgs TResult? whenOrNull<TResult extends Object?>({TResult? Function()?  initial,TResult? Function()?  loading,TResult? Function( List<PendingRating> pendingRatings)?  loaded,TResult? Function()?  submissionSuccess,TResult? Function( String message)?  error,}) {final _that = this;
switch (_that) {
case _Initial() when initial != null:
return initial();case _Loading() when loading != null:
return loading();case _Loaded() when loaded != null:
return loaded(_that.pendingRatings);case _SubmissionSuccess() when submissionSuccess != null:
return submissionSuccess();case _Error() when error != null:
return error(_that.message);case _:
  return null;

}
}

}

@freezed
sealed class PendingRatingEvent with _$PendingRatingEvent {
  /// Dispatched to load the initial list of pending ratings.
  const factory PendingRatingEvent.fetchList() = _FetchList;

  /// Dispatched when the user submits a rating for a specific user/session.
  const factory PendingRatingEvent.submitRating({
    required String sessionId,
    required String ratedUserId,
    required int ratingValue,
    String? comment,
  }) = _SubmitRating;
}

part of 'pending_rating_bloc.dart';

@freezed
sealed class PendingRatingState with _$PendingRatingState {
  /// The initial state, before anything is loaded.
  const factory PendingRatingState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// The state while fetching the list or submitting a rating.
  const factory PendingRatingState.loading() = _Loading;

  /// The state when the list of pending ratings is successfully loaded.
  const factory PendingRatingState.loaded({
    required List<PendingRating> pendingRatings,
  }) = _Loaded;

  /// A temporary state to indicate one rating was submitted successfully.
  /// The BLoC will likely transition from this back to 'loaded' with an updated list.
  const factory PendingRatingState.submissionSuccess() = _SubmissionSuccess;

  /// The state when an error occurs.
  const factory PendingRatingState.error(String message) = _Error;
}

import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/entities/pending_rating.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/presentation/bloc/pending_rating_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/presentation/widgets/create_rating_dialog.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/app_bars/custom_app_bar.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/states/loading_states.dart';

@RoutePage()
class PendingRatingsPage extends StatelessWidget {
  const PendingRatingsPage({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocProvider(
      create: (context) =>
          getIt<PendingRatingBloc>()..add(const PendingRatingEvent.fetchList()),
      child: const PendingRatingsView(),
    );
  }
}

class PendingRatingsView extends StatelessWidget {
  const PendingRatingsView({super.key});

  void _showRatingDialog(BuildContext context, PendingRating rating) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true, // Allows the sheet to resize for the keyboard
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (dialogContext) {
        // Provide the BLoC from the page's context to the dialog
        return BlocProvider.value(
          value: context.read<PendingRatingBloc>(),
          child: CreateRatingDialog(
            pendingRating: rating,
            onSubmit: (ratingValue, comment) {
              // The dialog calls this, and we add the event to the BLoC
              context.read<PendingRatingBloc>().add(
                    PendingRatingEvent.submitRating(
                      sessionId: rating.sessionId,
                      ratedUserId: rating.userIdToRate,
                      ratingValue: ratingValue,
                      comment: comment,
                    ),
                  );
            },
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: const CustomAppBar(title: 'Pending Ratings'),
      body: BlocConsumer<PendingRatingBloc, PendingRatingState>(
        listener: (context, state) {
          state.maybeWhen(
            submissionSuccess: () {
              // On success, close the bottom sheet
              Navigator.of(context).pop();
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                const SnackBar(
                  content: Text('Rating submitted successfully!'),
                  backgroundColor: AppTheme.successGreen,
                ),
              );
            },
            error: (message) {
              // Show error messages in a snackbar
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(
                  content: Text(message),
                  backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
                ),
              );
            },
            orElse: () {},
          );
        },
        builder: (context, state) {
          return state.when(
            initial: () => const LoadingIndicator(),
            // Show loading for both list fetching and rating submission
            loading: () => const LoadingIndicator(),
            submissionSuccess: () =>
                const LoadingIndicator(), // Transient state
            error: (message) => ErrorStateWidget(
              message: message,
              buttonText: 'Retry',
              onRetry: () {
                context
                    .read<PendingRatingBloc>()
                    .add(const PendingRatingEvent.fetchList());
              },
            ),
            loaded: (pendingRatings) {
              if (pendingRatings.isEmpty) {
                return const Center(
                  child: EmptyStateWidget(
                    icon: Icons.rate_review_outlined,
                    title: 'All Caught Up!',
                    message: 'You have no pending ratings to submit.',
                  ),
                );
              }

              return ListView.separated(
                itemCount: pendingRatings.length,
                separatorBuilder: (context, index) =>
                    const Divider(height: 1, indent: 16, endIndent: 16),
                itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                  final rating = pendingRatings[index];
                  return _PendingRatingCard(
                    rating: rating,
                    onTap: () => _showRatingDialog(context, rating),
                  );
                },
              );
            },
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// A local widget to display a single pending rating item in the list.
class _PendingRatingCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final PendingRating rating;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const _PendingRatingCard({
    required this.rating,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ListTile(
      leading: const Icon(Icons.person_outline, color: AppTheme.primaryRed),
      title: Text(
        'Rate ${rating.userNameToRate}',
        style: context.textTheme.titleMedium,
      ),
      subtitle: Text(
        'For session: ${rating.sessionTitle}',
        style: context.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
          color: AppTheme.disabledGray,
        ),
      ),
      trailing: const Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios_rounded, size: 16),
      onTap: onTap,
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/domain/entities/pending_rating.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/presentation/bloc/pending_rating_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/rating/presentation/widgets/rating_stars.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/buttons/custom_buttons.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/inputs/custom_inputs.dart';

class CreateRatingDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  final PendingRating pendingRating;
  final Function(int rating, String? comment) onSubmit;

  const CreateRatingDialog({
    super.key,
    required this.pendingRating,
    required this.onSubmit,
  });

  @override
  State<CreateRatingDialog> createState() => _CreateRatingDialogState();
}

class _CreateRatingDialogState extends State<CreateRatingDialog> {
  int _rating = 0;
  final _commentController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _commentController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _submit() {
    if (_rating == 0) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('Please select a rating (1-5 stars).'),
          backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    widget.onSubmit(_rating, _commentController.text.trim());
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // Listen to the BLoC to know if we are in a loading state
    final bool isLoading = context
        .watch<PendingRatingBloc>()
        .state // <-- Fix applied here
        .maybeWhen(loading: () => true, orElse: () => false);

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      // Padding to avoid the keyboard
      padding:
          EdgeInsets.only(bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom),
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppTheme.defaultPadding),
        decoration: const BoxDecoration(
          color: AppTheme.cleanWhite,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(
            topLeft: Radius.circular(16),
            topRight: Radius.circular(16),
          ),
        ),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(
              'Rate ${widget.pendingRating.userNameToRate}',
              style: context.textTheme.headlineSmall,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              'For session: ${widget.pendingRating.sessionTitle}',
              style: context.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                color: AppTheme.disabledGray,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            RatingStars(
              onRatingChanged: (rating) {
                setState(() {
                  _rating = rating;
                });
              },
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            PrimaryTextField(
              label: 'Add a comment (Optional)',
              controller: _commentController,
              maxLines: 3,
              hintText: 'How was your session with this user?',
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            PrimaryButton(
              text: 'Submit Rating',
              onPressed: _submit,
              isLoading: isLoading,
              // Disable button if no rating is selected or if loading
              enabled: _rating > 0 && !isLoading,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';

class RatingStars extends StatefulWidget {
  final int initialRating;
  final Function(int) onRatingChanged;

  const RatingStars({
    super.key,
    this.initialRating = 0,
    required this.onRatingChanged,
  });

  @override
  State<RatingStars> createState() => _RatingStarsState();
}

class _RatingStarsState extends State<RatingStars> {
  late int _rating;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _rating = widget.initialRating;
  }

  Widget _buildStar(int index) {
    // isFilled is true if the star's index is less than the current rating
    final bool isFilled = _rating >= index + 1;

    IconData icon =
        isFilled ? Icons.star_rounded : Icons.star_border_rounded;
    Color color = isFilled ? AppTheme.warningYellow : AppTheme.disabledGray;

    return IconButton(
      onPressed: () {
        setState(() {
          _rating = index + 1;
        });
        widget.onRatingChanged(_rating);
      },
      icon: Icon(
        icon,
        color: color,
        size: 36, // Make stars a good size for tapping
      ),
      splashColor: AppTheme.warningYellow.withOpacity(0.1),
      highlightColor: AppTheme.warningYellow.withOpacity(0.1),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
      children: List.generate(5, (index) => _buildStar(index)),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/api/api_client.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/constants/api_endpoints.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/report/data/models/create_report_model.dart';

abstract class ReportRemoteDatasource {
  Future<void> createReport(CreateReportModel request);
}

@Injectable(as: ReportRemoteDatasource)
class ReportRemoteDatasourceImpl implements ReportRemoteDatasource {
  final ApiClient _apiClient;

  ReportRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._apiClient);

  @override
  Future<void> createReport(CreateReportModel request) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.reports,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/report/data/datasources/report_remote_datasource.dart';

import 'package:sahbifit/features/report/domain/repositories/report_repository.dart';

@Injectable(as: ReportRepository)
class ReportRepositoryImpl implements ReportRepository {
  final ReportRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;

  ReportRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource);

  @override
  Future<void> createReport({
    required String reportedUserId,
    required String reason,
    String? sessionId,
  }) async {
    try {
      final requestModel = CreateReportModel(
        reportedUserId: reportedUserId,
        reason: reason,
        sessionId: sessionId,
      );
      await _remoteDatasource.createReport(requestModel);
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/report/domain/repositories/report_repository.dart';

@injectable
class CreateReport implements UseCase<void, CreateReportParams> {
  final ReportRepository _repository;

  CreateReport(this._repository);

  @override
  Future<void> call(CreateReportParams params) async {
    return _repository.createReport(
      reportedUserId: params.reportedUserId,
      reason: params.reason,
      sessionId: params.sessionId,
    );
  }
}

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/report/domain/usecases/create_report.dart';

part 'report_cubit.freezed.dart';
part 'report_state.dart';

@injectable
class ReportCubit extends Cubit<ReportState> {
  final CreateReport _createReport;

  ReportCubit(this._createReport) : super(const ReportState.initial());

  /// Submits a user report to the server.
  Future<void> submitReport({
    required String reportedUserId,
    required String reason,
    String? sessionId,
  }) async {
    emit(const ReportState.loading());
    try {
      final params = CreateReportParams(
        reportedUserId: reportedUserId,
        reason: reason,
        sessionId: sessionId,
      );
      await _createReport(params);
      emit(const ReportState.success());
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(ReportState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ReportState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

@freezed
sealed class ReportState with _$ReportState {
  /// The initial, idle state of the report form.
  const factory ReportState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// The state when the report is being submitted to the API.
  const factory ReportState.loading() = _Loading;

  /// The state when the report has been successfully submitted.
  const factory ReportState.success() = _Success;

  /// The state when an error occurs during submission.
  const factory ReportState.error(String message) = _Error;
}

import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/report/presentation/cubit/report_cubit.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/report/presentation/widgets/report_form.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/app_bars/custom_app_bar.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/dialogs/custom_dialogs.dart';

@RoutePage()
class ReportUserPage extends StatelessWidget {
  /// The unique ID of the user being reported.
  final String reportedUserId;

  /// The session ID, if the report is related to a specific session.
  final String? sessionId;

  const ReportUserPage({
    super.key,
    required this.reportedUserId,
    this.sessionId,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocProvider(
      create: (_) => getIt<ReportCubit>(),
      child: BlocListener<ReportCubit, ReportState>(
        listener: (context, state) {
          state.mapOrNull(
            success: (_) {
              // Show a success bottom sheet and then navigate back
              showModalBottomSheet(
                context: context,
                isDismissible: false,
                builder: (ctx) => InfoBottomSheet(
                  title: 'Report Submitted',
                  message:
                      'Thank you for helping keep our community safe. We will review your report shortly.',
                  buttonText: 'Got It',
                  onButtonPressed: () {
                    // Pop the bottom sheet
                    Navigator.of(ctx).pop();
                    // Pop the ReportUserPage
                    context.router.pop();
                  },
                ),
              );
            },
            error: (errorState) {
              // Show an error snackbar
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(
                  content: Text(errorState.message),
                  backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error,
                ),
              );
            },
          );
        },
        child: Scaffold(
          appBar: const CustomAppBar(title: 'Report User'),
          body: BlocBuilder<ReportCubit, ReportState>(
            builder: (context, state) {
              final isLoading = state.maybeWhen(
                loading: () => true,
                orElse: () => false,
              );

              return ReportForm(
                isLoading: isLoading,
                onSubmit: (String reason) {
                  // Dispatch the event to the cubit
                  context.read<ReportCubit>().submitReport(
                        reportedUserId: reportedUserId,
                        reason: reason,
                        sessionId: sessionId,
                      );
                },
              );
            },
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/utils/validators.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/buttons/custom_buttons.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/inputs/custom_inputs.dart';

class ReportForm extends StatefulWidget {
  final bool isLoading;
  final Function(String reason) onSubmit;

  const ReportForm({
    super.key,
    required this.isLoading,
    required this.onSubmit,
  });

  @override
  State<ReportForm> createState() => _ReportFormState();
}

class _ReportFormState extends State<ReportForm> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _reasonController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _reasonController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _submitForm() {
    // Hide keyboard
    FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();

    // Validate the form
    if (_formKey.currentState?.validate() ?? false) {
      widget.onSubmit(_reasonController.text.trim());
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Form(
      key: _formKey,
      child: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppTheme.defaultPadding),
        children: [
          Text(
            'Please describe what happened',
            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.elementSpacing),
          Text(
            'Provide as much detail as possible so our team can review the situation. Your report is confidential.',
            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
          PrimaryTextField(
            controller: _reasonController,
            label: 'Reason for Report',
            hintText: 'Describe the issue...',
            maxLines: 8,
            maxLength: 500, // Limit the report length
            validator: (value) => validateNotEmpty(value, 'Reason'),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
          PrimaryButton(
            text: 'Submit Report',
            isLoading: widget.isLoading,
            onPressed: _submitForm,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/api/api_client.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/constants/api_endpoints.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/datasources/session_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/models/create_session_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/models/mark_attendance_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/models/session_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/enums/verified_gender_enum.dart';

@Injectable(as: SessionRemoteDatasource)
class SessionRemoteDatasourceImpl implements SessionRemoteDatasource {
  final ApiClient _apiClient;

  SessionRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._apiClient);

  @override
  Future<List<SessionModel>> getActiveSessions(VerifiedGender? genderFilter) async {
    try {
      final Map<String, dynamic> queryParams = {};
      if (genderFilter != null) {
        queryParams['genderFilter'] = genderFilter.name;
      }

      final response = await _apiClient.dio.get(
        ApiEndpoints.sessions,
        queryParameters: queryParams.isNotEmpty ? queryParams : null,
      );

      return (response.data as List)
          .map((json) => SessionModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<SessionModel> createSession(CreateSessionModel request) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.createSession,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
      // Backend returns the created session
      return SessionModel.fromJson(response.data);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> joinSession(String sessionId) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.joinSession(sessionId), // Use function for dynamic URL
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> markAttendance(MarkAttendanceModel request) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.put(
        ApiEndpoints.markAttendance,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }
}
