import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/data/models/gym_model.dart'; // We'll re-use the GymModel
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/entities/session.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/enums/session_status.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/enums/session_type.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/data/models/user_model.dart'; // We'll re-use the UserModel

part 'session_model.freezed.dart';
part 'session_model.g.dart';


@freezed
abstract class SessionModel with _$SessionModel {
  const SessionModel._(); // Private constructor for methods

  
  const factory SessionModel({
    required String sessionId,
    required String title,
    required SessionType sessionType,
    required SessionStatus status,
    required String startTime, // Receive as ISO 8601 String
    required String endTime,   // Receive as ISO 8601 String
    required int maxParticipants,
    required int participantCount,
    required UserModel creator, // Nested JSON object
    required GymModel gym,       // Nested JSON object
  }) = _SessionModel;

  factory SessionModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$SessionModelFromJson(json);

  // Converts the data-layer model to a domain-layer entity
  Session toEntity() {
    return Session(
      sessionId: sessionId,
      title: title,
      sessionType: sessionType,
      status: status,
      startTime: DateTime.parse(startTime), // Convert String to DateTime
      endTime: DateTime.parse(endTime),     // Convert String to DateTime
      maxParticipants: maxParticipants,
      participantCount: participantCount,
      creator: creator.toEntity(), // Convert nested model to entity
      gym: gym.toEntity(),         // Convert nested model to entity
    );
  }
}

import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/datasources/session_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/models/create_session_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/models/mark_attendance_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/entities/session.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/enums/participant_status.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/repositories/session_repository.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/enums/verified_gender_enum.dart';

@Injectable(as: SessionRepository)
class SessionRepositoryImpl implements SessionRepository {
  final SessionRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;

  SessionRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource);

  @override
  Future<List<Session>> getActiveSessions(VerifiedGender? genderFilter) async {
    try {
      final sessionModels = await _remoteDatasource.getActiveSessions(genderFilter);
      return sessionModels.map((model) => model.toEntity()).toList();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<Session> createSession(CreateSessionModel request) async {
    try {
      final sessionModel = await _remoteDatasource.createSession(request);
      return sessionModel.toEntity();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> joinSession(String sessionId) async {
    try {
      await _remoteDatasource.joinSession(sessionId);
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> markAttendance(String sessionId, String userId, ParticipantStatus status) async {
    try {
      final requestModel = MarkAttendanceModel(
        sessionId: sessionId,
        participantUserId: userId,
        status: status,
      );
      await _remoteDatasource.markAttendance(requestModel);
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/models/create_session_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/entities/session.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/enums/session_type.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/repositories/session_repository.dart';

@injectable
class CreateSession implements UseCase<Session, CreateSessionParams> {
  final SessionRepository _repository;

  CreateSession(this._repository);

  @override
  Future<Session> call(CreateSessionParams params) async {
    // Map from clean params to the data model
    final requestModel = CreateSessionModel(
      gymId: params.gymId,
      sessionType: params.sessionType,
      title: params.title,
      startTime: params.startTime.toIso8601String(), // Convert DateTime to String
      endTime: params.endTime.toIso8601String(),     // Convert DateTime to String
      maxParticipants: params.maxParticipants,
    );
    return _repository.createSession(requestModel);
  }
}

class CreateSessionParams extends Equatable {
  final String gymId;
  final SessionType sessionType;
  final String title;
  final DateTime startTime;
  final DateTime endTime;
  final int maxParticipants;

  const CreateSessionParams({
    required this.gymId,
    required this.sessionType,
    required this.title,
    required this.startTime,
    required this.endTime,
    required this.maxParticipants,
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
        gymId,
        sessionType,
        title,
        startTime,
        endTime,
        maxParticipants,
      ];

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/entities/gym.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/entities/session.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/enums/session_type.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/usecases/create_session.dart';

part 'create_session_cubit.freezed.dart';
part 'create_session_state.dart';

@injectable
class CreateSessionCubit extends Cubit<CreateSessionState> {
  final CreateSession _createSession;

  CreateSessionCubit(this._createSession)
      : super(CreateSessionState.initial(
          // Default to 1 hour from now, duration 1 hour
          startTime: DateTime.now().add(const Duration(hours: 1)),
          endTime: DateTime.now().add(const Duration(hours: 2)),
        ));

  void titleChanged(String title) {
    emit(state.copyWith(title: title, status: CreateSessionStatus.initial, errorMessage: null));
  }

  void gymChanged(Gym gym) {
    emit(state.copyWith(selectedGym: gym, status: CreateSessionStatus.initial, errorMessage: null));
  }

  void sessionTypeChanged(SessionType type) {
    emit(state.copyWith(sessionType: type, status: CreateSessionStatus.initial, errorMessage: null));
  }

  void startTimeChanged(DateTime time) {
    emit(state.copyWith(startTime: time, status: CreateSessionStatus.initial, errorMessage: null));
  }

  void endTimeChanged(DateTime time) {
    emit(state.copyWith(endTime: time, status: CreateSessionStatus.initial, errorMessage: null));
  }

  void maxParticipantsChanged(int count) {
    emit(state.copyWith(maxParticipants: count, status: CreateSessionStatus.initial, errorMessage: null));
  }

  Future<void> submitCreateSession() async {
    if (state.selectedGym == null || state.title.trim().isEmpty) {
      emit(state.copyWith(
          status: CreateSessionStatus.error,
          errorMessage: 'Title and Gym are required.'));
      return;
    }

    if (state.endTime.isBefore(state.startTime.add(const Duration(minutes: 30)))) {
       emit(state.copyWith(
          status: CreateSessionStatus.error,
          errorMessage: 'End time must be at least 30 minutes after start time.'));
      return;
    }

    emit(state.copyWith(status: CreateSessionStatus.loading, errorMessage: null));
    try {
      final params = CreateSessionParams(
        gymId: state.selectedGym!.gymId,
        sessionType: state.sessionType,
        title: state.title.trim(),
        startTime: state.startTime,
        endTime: state.endTime,
        maxParticipants: state.maxParticipants,
      );
      final session = await _createSession(params);
      emit(state.copyWith(
          status: CreateSessionStatus.success, createdSession: session));
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(
          state.copyWith(status: CreateSessionStatus.error, errorMessage: e.message));
    } catch(e) {
       emit(
          state.copyWith(status: CreateSessionStatus.error, errorMessage: e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

enum CreateSessionStatus { initial, loading, success, error }


@freezed
sealed class CreateSessionState with _$CreateSessionState {
  const factory CreateSessionState({
    @Default(CreateSessionStatus.initial) CreateSessionStatus status,
    @Default('') String title,
    Gym? selectedGym,
    @Default(SessionType.group) SessionType sessionType,
    required DateTime startTime,
    required DateTime endTime,
    @Default(3) int maxParticipants,
    String? errorMessage,
    Session? createdSession,
  }) = _CreateSessionState;

  factory CreateSessionState.initial(
          {required DateTime startTime, required DateTime endTime}) =>
      CreateSessionState(startTime: startTime, endTime: endTime);
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/models/session_participant_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/entities/session.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/entities/session_participant.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/enums/participant_status.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/usecases/get_active_sessions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/usecases/mark_attendance.dart';

part 'mark_attendance_cubit.freezed.dart';
part 'mark_attendance_state.dart';

@injectable
class MarkAttendanceCubit extends Cubit<MarkAttendanceState> {
  final GetActiveSessions _getActiveSessions;
  final MarkAttendance _markAttendance;
  final FirebaseFirestore _firestore;

  StreamSubscription? _participantsSubscription;

  MarkAttendanceCubit(
    this._getActiveSessions,
    this._markAttendance,
    this._firestore,
  ) : super(const MarkAttendanceState.initial());

  Future<void> loadSession(String sessionId) async {
    emit(const MarkAttendanceState.loading());
    try {
      // 1. Fetch base session data
      final sessions = await _getActiveSessions(const GetActiveSessionsParams());
      final session = sessions.firstWhere((s) => s.sessionId == sessionId);

      // 2. Listen for participants
      _participantsSubscription?.cancel();
      _participantsSubscription = _firestore
          .collection('sessions')
          .doc(sessionId)
          .collection('participants')
          .snapshots()
          .listen((snapshot) {
        final participants = snapshot.docs
            .map((doc) => SessionParticipantModel.fromJson(doc.data()).toEntity())
            .toList();
        emit(MarkAttendanceState.loaded(
          session: session,
          participants: participants,
        ));
      });
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(MarkAttendanceState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(MarkAttendanceState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> submitAttendance(String userId, ParticipantStatus status) async {
    final currentState = state;
    if (currentState is _Loaded) {
      emit(currentState.copyWith(isSubmitting: true));
      try {
        await _markAttendance(MarkAttendanceParams(
          sessionId: currentState.session.sessionId,
          userId: userId,
          status: status,
        ));
        // The stream will refresh the list, so we just stop loading
        emit(currentState.copyWith(isSubmitting: false));
      } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
        // Show error on the loaded state
        emit(currentState.copyWith(isSubmitting: false, errorMessage: e.message));
        // Clear error after a few seconds
        await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 3));
        emit(currentState.copyWith(errorMessage: null));
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> close() {
    _participantsSubscription?.cancel();
    return super.close();
  }
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/app/bloc/app_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/data/models/session_participant_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/entities/session.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/entities/session_participant.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/usecases/get_active_sessions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/usecases/join_session.dart';

part 'session_details_cubit.freezed.dart';
part 'session_details_state.dart';

@injectable
class SessionDetailsCubit extends Cubit<SessionDetailsState> {
  final GetActiveSessions _getActiveSessions;
  final JoinSession _joinSession;
  final FirebaseFirestore _firestore;
  final AppBloc _appBloc;

  StreamSubscription? _participantsSubscription;

  SessionDetailsCubit(
    this._getActiveSessions,
    this._joinSession,
    this._firestore,
    this._appBloc,
  ) : super(const SessionDetailsState.initial());

  Future<void> fetchSessionDetails(String sessionId) async {
    emit(const SessionDetailsState.loading());
    try {
      // 1. Get current user ID from AppBloc
      final currentUserId = _appBloc.state.mapOrNull(
        authenticated: (s) => s.user.userId,
      );
      if (currentUserId == null) {
        emit(const SessionDetailsState.error('Not authenticated'));
        return;
      }

      // 2. Fetch base session data (Workaround: fetching all and filtering)
      // A dedicated GetSessionById use case would be better.
      final sessions = await _getActiveSessions(const GetActiveSessionsParams());
      final session = sessions.firstWhere((s) => s.sessionId == sessionId);

      // 3. Listen for participants from Firestore
      _participantsSubscription?.cancel();
      _participantsSubscription = _firestore
          .collection('sessions')
          .doc(sessionId)
          .collection('participants')
          .snapshots()
          .listen((snapshot) {
        final participants = snapshot.docs
            .map((doc) => SessionParticipantModel.fromJson(doc.data()).toEntity())
            .toList();

        final isJoined = participants.any((p) => p.userId == currentUserId);

        emit(SessionDetailsState.loaded(
          session: session,
          participants: participants,
          isJoined: isJoined,
        ));
      }, onError: (e) {
        emit(SessionDetailsState.error(
            'Failed to load participants: ${e.toString()}'));
      });
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(SessionDetailsState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(SessionDetailsState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> joinSession(String sessionId) async {
    state.maybeMap(
      loaded: (s) async {
        emit(s.copyWith(isJoinLoading: true));
        try {
          await _joinSession(JoinSessionParams(sessionId: sessionId));
          // The stream listener will automatically update the state,
          // so we just need to wait for it.
          // We'll reset loading state when the stream updates.
        } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
          // Emit an error but keep the loaded state
          emit(s.copyWith(
            isJoinLoading: false,
            errorMessage: e.message,
          ));
          // Remove the error message after a delay
          await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 3));
          emit(s.copyWith(errorMessage: null));
        }
      },
      orElse: () {
        // Not in a loaded state, do nothing
      },
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<void> close() {
    _participantsSubscription?.cancel();
    return super.close();
  }
}

@freezed
sealed class SessionDetailsState with _$SessionDetailsState {
  const factory SessionDetailsState.initial() = _Initial;
  const factory SessionDetailsState.loading() = _Loading;
  const factory SessionDetailsState.loaded({
    required Session session,
    required List<SessionParticipant> participants,
    required bool isJoined,
    @Default(false) bool isJoinLoading,
    String? errorMessage,
  }) = _Loaded;
  const factory SessionDetailsState.error(String message) = _Error;
}

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/entities/session.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/usecases/get_active_sessions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/enums/verified_gender_enum.dart';

part 'session_feed_cubit.freezed.dart';
part 'session_feed_state.dart';

@injectable
class SessionFeedCubit extends Cubit<SessionFeedState> {
  final GetActiveSessions _getActiveSessions;

  SessionFeedCubit(this._getActiveSessions)
      : super(const SessionFeedState.initial());

  /// Fetches the list of active sessions, optionally applying a gender filter.
  Future<void> fetchSessions({VerifiedGender? genderFilter}) async {
    emit(const SessionFeedState.loading());
    try {
      final params = GetActiveSessionsParams(genderFilter: genderFilter);
      final sessions = await _getActiveSessions(params);

      emit(SessionFeedState.loaded(
        sessions: sessions,
        genderFilter: genderFilter,
      ));
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(SessionFeedState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(SessionFeedState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

part of 'session_feed_cubit.dart';

@freezed
sealed class SessionFeedState with _$SessionFeedState {
  /// The initial state, before any sessions are fetched.
  const factory SessionFeedState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// The state while sessions are being fetched from the API.
  const factory SessionFeedState.loading() = _Loading;

  /// The state when sessions have been successfully loaded.
  const factory SessionFeedState.loaded({
    required List<Session> sessions,
    VerifiedGender? genderFilter, // Store the filter that was used
  }) = _Loaded;

  /// The state when an error occurs during fetching.
  const factory SessionFeedState.error(String message) = _Error;
}

import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_router.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/domain/entities/gym.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/gym/presentation/cubit/gym_list_cubit.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/presentation/cubit/create_session_cubit.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/presentation/widgets/session_type_toggle.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/app_bars/custom_app_bar.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/buttons/custom_buttons.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/inputs/custom_inputs.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/states/loading_states.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/utils/date_formatters.dart';

@AutoRoutePage()
class CreateSessionPage extends StatelessWidget {
  const CreateSessionPage({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MultiBlocProvider(
      providers: [
        BlocProvider(create: (context) => getIt<CreateSessionCubit>()),
        BlocProvider(create: (context) => getIt<GymListCubit>()..fetchApprovedGyms()),
      ],
      child: BlocListener<CreateSessionCubit, CreateSessionState>(
        listener: (context, state) {
          if (state.status == CreateSessionStatus.success) {
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
              const SnackBar(
                content: Text('Session created successfully!'),
                backgroundColor: AppTheme.successGreen,
              ),
            );
            // Navigate to the details page of the new session
            context.router.replace(
                SessionDetailsRoute(sessionId: state.createdSession!.sessionId));
          } else if (state.status == CreateSessionStatus.error) {
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
              SnackBar(
                content: Text(state.errorMessage ?? 'An error occurred.'),
                backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
              ),
            );
          }
        },
        child: Scaffold(
          appBar: const CustomAppBar(title: 'Create Session'),
          body: SingleChildScrollView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppTheme.defaultPadding),
            child: _CreateSessionForm(),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _CreateSessionForm extends StatelessWidget {
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final cubit = context.watch<CreateSessionCubit>();
    final state = cubit.state;

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        PrimaryTextField(
          label: 'Session Title',
          hintText: 'e.g., Morning Chest Day',
          onChanged: cubit.titleChanged,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
        Text('Session Type', style: context.textTheme.titleMedium),
        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.elementSpacing),
        SessionTypeToggle(
          selectedType: state.sessionType,
          onTypeChanged: cubit.sessionTypeChanged,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
        Text('Location', style: context.textTheme.titleMedium),
        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.elementSpacing),
        _GymSelector(),
        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
        // Simple Date/Time Pickers (a real app might use better pickers)
        ElevatedButton(
          onPressed: () async {
            final date = await showDatePicker(
              context: context,
              initialDate: state.startTime,
              firstDate: DateTime.now(),
              lastDate: DateTime.now().add(const Duration(days: 30)),
            );
            if (date != null && context.mounted) {
              final time = await showTimePicker(
                context: context,
                initialTime: TimeOfDay.fromDateTime(state.startTime),
              );
              if (time != null) {
                final newStartTime = DateTime(
                    date.year, date.month, date.day, time.hour, time.minute);
                cubit.startTimeChanged(newStartTime);
              }
            }
          },
          child: Text(
              'Start: ${formatReadableDateTime(state.startTime.toIso8601String())}'),
        ),
        ElevatedButton(
          onPressed: () async {
             final date = await showDatePicker(
              context: context,
              initialDate: state.endTime,
              firstDate: DateTime.now(),
              lastDate: DateTime.now().add(const Duration(days: 30)),
            );
            if (date != null && context.mounted) {
              final time = await showTimePicker(
                context: context,
                initialTime: TimeOfDay.fromDateTime(state.endTime),
              );
              if (time != null) {
                final newEndTime = DateTime(
                    date.year, date.month, date.day, time.hour, time.minute);
                cubit.endTimeChanged(newEndTime);
              }
            }
          },
          child: Text(
              'End: ${formatReadableDateTime(state.endTime.toIso8601String())}'),
        ),

        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
        Text('Max Participants: ${state.maxParticipants}',
            style: context.textTheme.titleMedium),
        Slider(
          value: state.maxParticipants.toDouble(),
          min: 2,
          max: 10,
          divisions: 8,
          label: state.maxParticipants.toString(),
          onChanged: (value) => cubit.maxParticipantsChanged(value.toInt()),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
        PrimaryButton(
          text: 'Create Session',
          isLoading: state.status == CreateSessionStatus.loading,
          onPressed: cubit.submitCreateSession,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _GymSelector extends StatelessWidget {
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final createCubit = context.read<CreateSessionCubit>();
    return BlocBuilder<GymListCubit, GymListState>(
      builder: (context, state) {
        return state.map(
          initial: (_) => const LoadingIndicator(),
          loading: (_) => const LoadingIndicator(),
          error: (e) => Text('Error loading gyms: ${e.message}'),
          loaded: (s) {
            return DropdownButtonFormField<Gym>(
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'Select Gym',
              ),
              items: s.gyms
                  .map((gym) => DropdownMenuItem(
                        value: gym,
                        child: Text(gym.name),
                      ))
                  .toList(),
              onChanged: (gym) {
                if (gym != null) {
                  createCubit.gymChanged(gym);
                }
              },
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/presentation/cubit/mark_attendance_cubit.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/presentation/widgets/session_participant_list.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/app_bars/custom_app_bar.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/states/loading_states.dart';

@AutoRoutePage()
class MarkAttendancePage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String sessionId;

  const MarkAttendancePage({
    super.key,
    @PathParam('sessionId') required this.sessionId,
  });

  @override
  State<MarkAttendancePage> createState() => _MarkAttendancePageState();
}

class _MarkAttendancePageState extends State<MarkAttendancePage> {
  late final MarkAttendanceCubit _cubit;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _cubit = getIt<MarkAttendanceCubit>()..loadSession(widget.sessionId);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _cubit.close();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocProvider.value(
      value: _cubit,
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: const CustomAppBar(title: 'Mark Attendance'),
        body: BlocConsumer<MarkAttendanceCubit, MarkAttendanceState>(
          listener: (context, state) {
            state.maybeMap(
              loaded: (s) {
                if (s.errorMessage != null) {
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    SnackBar(
                        content: Text(s.errorMessage!),
                        backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed),
                  );
                }
              },
              orElse: () {},
            );
          },
          builder: (context, state) {
            return state.map(
              initial: (_) => const LoadingIndicator(),
              loading: (_) => const LoadingIndicator(),
              error: (e) => ErrorStateWidget(
                message: e.message,
                buttonText: 'Try Again',
                onRetry: () => _cubit.loadSession(widget.sessionId),
              ),
              loaded: (s) {
                return SingleChildScrollView(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppTheme.defaultPadding),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(s.session.title,
                          style: context.textTheme.headlineMedium),
                      const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.elementSpacing),
                      Text(
                        'Mark participants as "Attended" or "No Show". This affects their reliability rating.',
                        style: context.textTheme.bodyMedium,
                      ),
                      const Divider(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
                      if (s.isSubmitting) const LinearProgressIndicator(),
                      SessionParticipantList(
                        participants: s.participants,
                        onMarkAttendance: (userId, status) {
                          _cubit.submitAttendance(userId, status);
                        },
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                );
              },
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

@AutoRoutePage()
class SessionDetailsPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String sessionId;

  const SessionDetailsPage({
    super.key,
    @PathParam('sessionId') required this.sessionId,
  });

  @override
  State<SessionDetailsPage> createState() => _SessionDetailsPageState();

class _SessionDetailsPageState extends State<SessionDetailsPage> {
  late final SessionDetailsCubit _cubit;
  late final User _currentUser;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _currentUser = context.read<AppBloc>().state.mapOrNull(
          authenticated: (s) => s.user,
        )!; // Must be authenticated
    _cubit = getIt<SessionDetailsCubit>()
      ..fetchSessionDetails(widget.sessionId);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _cubit.close();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocProvider.value(
      value: _cubit,
      child: Scaffold(
        body: BlocConsumer<SessionDetailsCubit, SessionDetailsState>(
          listener: (context, state) {
            state.maybeMap(
              loaded: (s) {
                if (s.errorMessage != null) {
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    SnackBar(
                        content: Text(s.errorMessage!),
                        backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed),
                  );
                }
              },
              orElse: () {},
            );
          },
          builder: (context, state) {
            return state.map(
              initial: (_) => const LoadingIndicator(),
              loading: (_) => const LoadingIndicator(),
              error: (e) => Center(
                child: ErrorStateWidget(
                  message: e.message,
                  buttonText: 'Try Again',
                  onRetry: () => _cubit.fetchSessionDetails(widget.sessionId),
                ),
              ),
              loaded: (s) => _buildLoadedBody(context, s),
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLoadedBody(BuildContext context, _Loaded state) {
    final session = state.session;
    final isCreator = session.creator.userId == _currentUser.userId;

    return CustomScrollView(
      slivers: [
        CustomAppBar(
          title: session.title,
        ),
        SliverToBoxAdapter(
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppTheme.defaultPadding),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                _buildInfoTile(
                  context,
                  icon: Icons.calendar_today_rounded,
                  title: 'Date & Time',
                  subtitle:
                      '${formatReadableDateTime(session.startTime.toIso8601String())}\n${formatReadableTime(session.endTime.toIso8601String())} (End)',
                ),
                _buildInfoTile(
                  context,
                  icon: Icons.location_on_rounded,
                  title: 'Location',
                  subtitle: session.gym.name,
                ),
                _buildInfoTile(
                  context,
                  icon: Icons.people_rounded,
                  title: 'Participants',
                  subtitle:
                      '${state.participants.length} / ${session.maxParticipants} joined',
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.elementSpacing),
                ListTile(
                  contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                  leading: const CircleAvatar(
                      // Placeholder
                      ),
                  title: Text('Hosted by ${session.creator.firstName}',
                      style: context.textTheme.titleMedium),
                  subtitle: const Text('View Profile'),
                  onTap: () => context.router
                      .push(ProfileRoute(userId: session.creator.userId)),
                ),
                const Divider(),
                const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.elementSpacing),
                Text('Who\'s Going', style: context.textTheme.headlineSmall),
                const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.elementSpacing),
                SessionParticipantList(participants: state.participants),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
        SliverFillRemaining(
          hasScrollBody: false,
          child: _buildBottomButton(context, state, isCreator),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildInfoTile(
    BuildContext context, {
    required IconData icon,
    required String title,
    required String subtitle,
  }) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8.0),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Icon(icon, color: AppTheme.primaryRed, size: 20),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(title, style: context.textTheme.titleMedium),
                const SizedBox(height: 2),
                Text(subtitle, style: context.textTheme.bodyMedium),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildBottomButton(
      BuildContext context, _Loaded state, bool isCreator) {
    final session = state.session;
    final bool isFull = state.participants.length >= session.maxParticipants;

    // Feature 3: Reputation Gate Logic
    // This assumes AppBloc's user entity has this info.
    final bool hasAttendedGroupSession =
        _currentUser.groupSessionCount > 0; // Example logic
    final bool isOneOnOne = session.sessionType == SessionType.oneOnOne;
    final bool isGated = isOneOnOne && !hasAttendedGroupSession;

    // Feature 3: Ephemeral Chat Logic
    final bool canChat =
        DateTime.now().isAfter(session.startTime.subtract(const Duration(hours: 1))) &&
            DateTime.now().isBefore(session.endTime.add(const Duration(hours: 1)));

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppTheme.defaultPadding),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: AppTheme.cleanWhite,
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.05),
            blurRadius: 10,
            offset: const Offset(0, -5),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          if (isCreator)
            PrimaryButton(
              text: 'Mark Attendance',
              onPressed: () => context.router
                  .push(MarkAttendanceRoute(sessionId: session.sessionId)),
            )
          else if (state.isJoined)
            PrimaryButton(
              text: canChat ? 'Open Chat' : 'Chat (Locked)',
              onPressed: canChat
                  ? () =>
                      context.push(ChatRoute(sessionId: session.sessionId))
                  : null,
            )
          else if (isFull)
            const PrimaryButton(
              text: 'Session Full',
              onPressed: null,
            )
          else
            PrimaryButton(
              text: isGated ? 'Join (Requires 1 Group Session)' : 'Join Session',
              isLoading: state.isJoinLoading,
              onPressed: isGated
                  ? () => showReputationGateDialog(context)
                  : () => _cubit.joinSession(session.sessionId),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/app/bloc/app_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_router.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/presentation/cubit/session_feed_cubit.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/presentation/widgets/session_card.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/enums/user_status.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/enums/user_role.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/app_bars/custom_app_bar.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/states/loading_states.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/enums/verification_status_enum.dart';

@AutoRoutePage()
class SessionFeedPage extends StatelessWidget {
  const SessionFeedPage({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // Assumes AppBloc is provided above this route
    final appState = context.watch<AppBloc>().state;
    final currentUser = appState.mapOrNull(authenticated: (s) => s.user);

    if (currentUser == null) {
      // This shouldn't happen if routing is correct, but good to guard
      return const Scaffold(body: LoadingIndicator(message: 'Authenticating...'));
    }

    return BlocProvider(
      create: (context) => getIt<SessionFeedCubit>()..fetchSessions(),
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: const CustomAppBar(
          title: 'Sessions',
          showBackButton: false,
          // You could add filter actions here
        ),
        body: BlocBuilder<SessionFeedCubit, SessionFeedState>(
          builder: (context, state) {
            return state.map(
              initial: (_) => const LoadingIndicator(),
              loading: (_) => const LoadingIndicator(),
              error: (e) => ErrorStateWidget(
                message: e.message,
                buttonText: 'Try Again',
                onRetry: () =>
                    context.read<SessionFeedCubit>().fetchSessions(),
              ),
              loaded: (s) {
                if (s.sessions.isEmpty) {
                  return Center(
                    child: EmptyStateWidget(
                      icon: Icons.search_off_rounded,
                      title: 'No Sessions Yet',
                      message:
                          'Looks quiet... Why not be the first to host one?',
                      buttonText: 'Create a Session',
                      onButtonPressed: () =>
                          context.push(const CreateSessionRoute()),
                    ),
                  );
                }
                return RefreshIndicator(
                  onRefresh: () =>
                      context.read<SessionFeedCubit>().fetchSessions(),
                  child: ListView.builder(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8, bottom: 80),
                    itemCount: s.sessions.length,
                    itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                      final session = s.sessions[index];

                      // Feature 2: Probationary Mode Logic
                      final bool isAmbassadorSession =
                          session.creator.role == UserRole.admin; // Placeholder logic
                      final bool isLocked =
                          currentUser.status == UserStatus.probationary &&
                              !isAmbassadorSession;

                      return SessionCardWrapper(
                        session: session,
                        isLocked: isLocked,
                      );
                    },
                  ),
                );
              },
            );
          },
        ),
        floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton(
          onPressed: () => context.push(const CreateSessionRoute()),
          child: const Icon(Icons.add),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

        Navigator.of(ctx).pop();
        // Here you would typically filter the feed.

        // Or navigate to a specific "ambassador" tab if it existed.
      },
    ),
  );
}

import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_router.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/dialogs/custom_dialogs.dart';

/// Shows an info bottom sheet explaining the "Reputation Gate"
/// for 1-on-1 sessions (Feature 3).
void showReputationGateDialog(BuildContext context) {
  showModalBottomSheet(
    context: context,
    builder: (ctx) => InfoBottomSheet(
      title: 'Unlock 1-on-1 Sessions',
      message:
          'To ensure community safety and reliability, you must attend at least one successful Group Session before you can post or join 1-on-1 sessions.',
      buttonText: 'Find a Group Session',
      onButtonPressed: () {
        Navigator.of(ctx).pop();
        // Navigate back to the feed, which defaults to Group sessions
        context.router.popUntil((route) => route.settings.name == SessionFeedRoute.name);
      },
    ),
  );
}

import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_router.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/utils/date_formatters.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/entities/session.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/presentation/widgets/probation_locked_tooltip.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/enums/user_role.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/cards/custom_cards.dart';

/// This widget is a wrapper that maps the domain [Session] entity
/// to the shared [SessionCard] widget, handling lock state and navigation.
class SessionCardWrapper extends StatelessWidget {
  final Session session;
  final bool isLocked;

  const SessionCardWrapper({
    super.key,
    required this.session,
    required this.isLocked,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // Check if the creator is an Ambassador (placeholder logic)
    // In a real app, this would check session.creator.badges
    final bool isAmbassadorSession =
        session.creator.role == UserRole.admin; // Assuming admin == ambassador for now

    final card = SessionCard(
      title: session.title,
      subtitle: 'Hosted by ${session.creator.firstName}',
      time: formatReadableTime(session.startTime.toIso8601String()),
      location: session.gym.name,
      participants: session.participantCount,
      maxParticipants: session.maxParticipants,
      isLocked: isLocked,
      isVerified: isAmbassadorSession, // Show "Verified" for ambassador sessions
      onTap: () {
        context.push(SessionDetailsRoute(sessionId: session.sessionId));
      },
    );

    if (isLocked) {
      return GestureDetector(
        onTap: () => showProbationLockedDialog(context),
        child: card,
      );
    } else {
      return card;
    }
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/entities/session_participant.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/enums/participant_status.dart';

class SessionParticipantList extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<SessionParticipant> participants;
  final Function(String userId, ParticipantStatus status)? onMarkAttendance;

  const SessionParticipantList({
    super.key,
    required this.participants,
    this.onMarkAttendance,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (participants.isEmpty) {
      return const Center(
        child: Padding(
          padding: EdgeInsets.all(32.0),
          child: Text(
            'No one has joined yet. Be the first!',
            style: AppTheme.lockedFeatureText,
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    return ListView.builder(
      shrinkWrap: true,
      physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
      itemCount: participants.length,
      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
        final participant = participants[index];
        return ListTile(
          leading: CircleAvatar(
            // Placeholder for profile pic
            backgroundColor: AppTheme.lightGray,
            child: Text(participant.user.firstName.substring(0, 1)),
          ),
          title: Text(
            participant.user.firstName,
            style: context.textTheme.titleMedium,
          ),
          subtitle: Text(
            'Status: ${participant.status.name}',
            style: context.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
              color: _getStatusColor(participant.status),
            ),
          ),
          trailing:
              onMarkAttendance != null ? _buildAttendanceButtons(context, participant) : null,
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildAttendanceButtons(BuildContext context, SessionParticipant participant) {
    return Row(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: [
        IconButton(
          icon: Icon(
            Icons.check_circle_rounded,
            color: participant.status == ParticipantStatus.attended
                ? AppTheme.successGreen
                : AppTheme.disabledGray,
          ),
          onPressed: () =>
              onMarkAttendance!(participant.userId, ParticipantStatus.attended),
        ),
        IconButton(
          icon: Icon(
            Icons.cancel_rounded,
            color: participant.status == ParticipantStatus.noShow
                ? AppTheme.primaryRed
                : AppTheme.disabledGray,
          ),
          onPressed: () =>
              onMarkAttendance!(participant.userId, ParticipantStatus.noShow),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Color _getStatusColor(ParticipantStatus status) {
    switch (status) {
      case ParticipantStatus.attended:
        return AppTheme.successGreen;
      case ParticipantStatus.noShow:
        return AppTheme.primaryRed;
      case ParticipantStatus.joined:
        return AppTheme.trustBlue;
      default:
        return AppTheme.disabledGray;
    }
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/utils/icon_utils.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/session/domain/enums/session_type.dart';

class SessionTypeToggle extends StatelessWidget {
  final SessionType selectedType;
  final ValueChanged<SessionType> onTypeChanged;

  const SessionTypeToggle({
    super.key,
    required this.selectedType,
    required this.onTypeChanged,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Expanded(
          child: _ToggleButton(
            label: 'Group Session',
            icon: getSessionTypeIcon(SessionType.group),
            isSelected: selectedType == SessionType.group,
            onTap: () => onTypeChanged(SessionType.group),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 12),
        Expanded(
          child: _ToggleButton(
            label: '1-on-1 Session',
            icon: getSessionTypeIcon(SessionType.oneOnOne),
            isSelected: selectedType == SessionType.oneOnOne,
            onTap: () => onTypeChanged(SessionType.oneOnOne),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _ToggleButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final IconData icon;
  final bool isSelected;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const _ToggleButton({
    required this.label,
    required this.icon,
    required this.isSelected,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final color = isSelected ? AppTheme.primaryRed : AppTheme.darkGray;
    final bgColor =
        isSelected ? AppTheme.primaryRed.withOpacity(0.1) : AppTheme.lightGray;

    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16, horizontal: 12),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: bgColor,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          border: Border.all(
            color: isSelected ? AppTheme.primaryRed : Colors.transparent,
            width: 1.5,
          ),
        ),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Icon(icon, color: color, size: 28),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              label,
              style: context.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                color: color,
                fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.w600 : FontWeight.w500,
              ),
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\Ahmed\Desktop\sahbifit\mobile\lib\features\tag\data\datasources\tag_remote_datasource.dart

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/api/api_client.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/constants/api_endpoints.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/data/models/tag_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag_type.dart';

abstract class TagRemoteDatasource {
  Future<List<TagModel>> getTagsByType(TagType type);
}

@Injectable(as: TagRemoteDatasource)
class TagRemoteDatasourceImpl implements TagRemoteDatasource {
  final ApiClient _apiClient;

  TagRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._apiClient);

  @override
  Future<List<TagModel>> getTagsByType(TagType type) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.get(
        ApiEndpoints.tags,
        queryParameters: {
          'type': type.name, // Sends ?type=goal or ?type=schedule
        },
      );

      // Convert the List<dynamic> from JSON to List<TagModel>
      return (response.data as List)
          .map((json) => TagModel.fromJson(json))
          .toList();
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag.dart';

part 'tag_model.freezed.dart';
part 'tag_model.g.dart';


@freezed
abstract class TagModel with _$TagModel {
  const TagModel._(); // Private constructor for methods

  
  const factory TagModel({
    required int tagId,
    required String tagName,
    required TagType tagType,
  }) = _TagModel;

  factory TagModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$TagModelFromJson(json);

  // Converts the data-layer model to a domain-layer entity
  Tag toEntity() {
    return Tag(
      tagId: tagId,
      tagName: tagName,
      tagType: tagType,
    );
  }
}

/// Adds pattern-matching-related methods to [TagModel].
extension TagModelPatterns on TagModel {
/// A variant of `map` that fallback to returning `orElse`.
///
/// It is equivalent to doing:
/// ```dart
/// switch (sealedClass) {
///   case final Subclass value:
///     return ...;
///   case _:
///     return orElse();
/// }
/// ```

import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/data/datasources/tag_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag_type.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/repositories/tag_repository.dart';

@Injectable(as: TagRepository)
class TagRepositoryImpl implements TagRepository {
  final TagRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;

  TagRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource);

  @override
  Future<List<Tag>> getTagsByType(TagType type) async {
    try {
      final tagModels = await _remoteDatasource.getTagsByType(type);
      // Map List<TagModel> to List<Tag>
      return tagModels.map((model) => model.toEntity()).toList();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag_type.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/repositories/tag_repository.dart';

@injectable
class GetTagsByType implements UseCase<List<Tag>, GetTagsByTypeParams> {
  final TagRepository _repository;

  GetTagsByType(this._repository);

  @override
  Future<List<Tag>> call(GetTagsByTypeParams params) async {
    return _repository.getTagsByType(params.type);
  }
}

class GetTagsByTypeParams extends Equatable {
  final TagType type;

  const GetTagsByTypeParams({required this.type});

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [type];
}

FILE: C:\Users\Ahmed\Desktop\sahbifit\mobile\lib\features\tag\presentation\cubit\tags_cubit.dart

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag_type.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/usecases/get_tags_by_type.dart';

part 'tags_cubit.freezed.dart';
part 'tags_state.dart';

@injectable
class TagsCubit extends Cubit<TagsState> {
  final GetTagsByType _getTagsByType;

  TagsCubit(this._getTagsByType) : super(const TagsState.initial());

  /// Fetches both GOAL and SCHEDULE tags concurrently.
  Future<void> loadAllTags() async {
    emit(const TagsState.loading());
    try {
      // Use Future.wait to fetch both lists in parallel
      final results = await Future.wait([
        _getTagsByType(const GetTagsByTypeParams(type: TagType.goal)),
        _getTagsByType(const GetTagsByTypeParams(type: TagType.schedule)),
      ]);

      // Extract the results
      final goalTags = results[0];
      final scheduleTags = results[1];

      emit(TagsState.loaded(
        goalTags: goalTags,
        scheduleTags: scheduleTags,
      ));
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(TagsState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(TagsState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

part of 'tags_cubit.dart';

@freezed
sealed class TagsState with _$TagsState {
  /// The initial state, before tags are fetched.
  const factory TagsState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// The state while fetching the tags from the API.
  const factory TagsState.loading() = _Loading;

  /// The state when both 'goal' and 'schedule' tags are successfully loaded.
  const factory TagsState.loaded({
    required List<Tag> goalTags,
    required List<Tag> scheduleTags,
  }) = _Loaded;

  /// The state when an error occurs during fetching.
  const factory TagsState.error(String message) = _Error;
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/tag/domain/entities/tag.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/badges/custom_badges.dart';

/// A reusable widget to display a list of tags as selectable chips.
///
/// This widget is used in the profile setup (for goals/schedule) and
/// when creating a session.
class TagSelectionWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  /// The title displayed above the chips (e.g., "Fitness Goals").
  final String title;

  /// The complete list of tags to display for this section.
  final List<Tag> allTags;

  /// The set of tags that are currently selected.
  final Set<Tag> selectedTags;

  /// Callback function triggered when any tag chip is tapped.
  /// The parent widget is responsible for updating the `selectedTags` set.
  final Function(Tag tag) onTagSelected;

  const TagSelectionWidget({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.allTags,
    required this.selectedTags,
    required this.onTagSelected,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        // Title for the section
        Text(
          title,
          style: context.textTheme.titleMedium,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.elementSpacing * 1.5),

        // Wrap for the chips
        Wrap(
          spacing: AppTheme.elementSpacing, // Horizontal space between chips
          runSpacing: AppTheme.elementSpacing, // Vertical space between lines
          children: allTags.map((tag) {
            final bool isSelected = selectedTags.contains(tag);
            return TagChip(
              label: tag.tagName,
              selected: isSelected,
              onTap: () => onTagSelected(tag),
            );
          }).toList(),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/api/api_client.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/constants/api_endpoints.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/data/models/update_fcm_token_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/data/models/update_visibility_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/data/models/user_model.dart';

abstract class UserRemoteDatasource {
  Future<UserModel> getMyProfile();
  Future<UserModel> getUserProfile(String userId);
  Future<void> blockUser(String userId);
  Future<void> updateFcmToken(UpdateFcmTokenModel request);
  Future<void> updateProfileVisibility(UpdateVisibilityModel request);
}

@Injectable(as: UserRemoteDatasource)
class UserRemoteDatasourceImpl implements UserRemoteDatasource {
  final ApiClient _apiClient;

  UserRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._apiClient);

  @override
  Future<UserModel> getMyProfile() async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.get(ApiEndpoints.myProfile);
      return UserModel.fromJson(response.data);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<UserModel> getUserProfile(String userId) async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.get(
        ApiEndpoints.getUserProfile(userId),
      );
      return UserModel.fromJson(response.data);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> blockUser(String userId) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.blockUser(userId),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> updateFcmToken(UpdateFcmTokenModel request) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.put(
        ApiEndpoints.updateFcmToken,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> updateProfileVisibility(UpdateVisibilityModel request) async {
    try {
      await _apiClient.dio.put(
        ApiEndpoints.updateVisibility,
        data: request.toJson(),
      );
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/entities/badge.dart';

part 'badge_model.freezed.dart';
part 'badge_model.g.dart';


@freezed
abstract class BadgeModel with _$BadgeModel {
  const BadgeModel._();

  
  const factory BadgeModel({
    required int badgeId,
    required String badgeName,
    required String description,
    String? iconUrl,
  }) = _BadgeModel;

  factory BadgeModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$BadgeModelFromJson(json);

  Badge toEntity() {
    return Badge(
      badgeId: badgeId,
      badgeName: badgeName,
      description: description,
      iconUrl: iconUrl,
    );
  }
}

@freezed
abstract class UserModel with _$UserModel {
  const UserModel._();

  
  const factory UserModel({
    required String userId,
    required String firstName,
    String? profilePhotoUrl,
    required VerificationStatus verificationStatus,
    required VerifiedGender verifiedGender,
    required ProfileVisibility profileVisibility,
    required UserRole role,
    required UserStatus status,
    required int groupSessionCount,
    required int noShowCount,
    GymModel? primaryGym,
    @Default([]) List<BadgeModel> userBadges,
  }) = _UserModel;

  factory UserModel.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
      _$UserModelFromJson(json);

  User toEntity() {
    return User(
      userId: userId,
      firstName: firstName,
      profilePhotoUrl: profilePhotoUrl,
      verificationStatus: verificationStatus,
      verifiedGender: verifiedGender,
      profileVisibility: profileVisibility,
      role: role,
      status: status,
      groupSessionCount: groupSessionCount,
      noShowCount: noShowCount,
      primaryGym: primaryGym?.toEntity(),
      userBadges: userBadges.map((badge) => badge.toEntity()).toList(),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/data/datasources/user_remote_datasource.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/data/models/update_fcm_token_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/data/models/update_visibility_model.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/entities/user.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/enums/profile_visibility.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/repositories/user_repository.dart';

@Injectable(as: UserRepository)
class UserRepositoryImpl implements UserRepository {
  final UserRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;

  UserRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource);

  @override
  Future<User> getMyProfile() async {
    try {
      final userModel = await _remoteDatasource.getMyProfile();
      return userModel.toEntity();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<User> getUserProfile(String userId) async {
    try {
      final userModel = await _remoteDatasource.getUserProfile(userId);
      return userModel.toEntity();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> blockUser(String userId) async {
    try {
      await _remoteDatasource.blockUser(userId);
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> updateFcmToken(String token) async {
    try {
      final requestModel = UpdateFcmTokenModel(fcmToken: token);
      await _remoteDatasource.updateFcmToken(requestModel);
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> updateProfileVisibility(ProfileVisibility visibility) async {
    try {
      final requestModel = UpdateVisibilityModel(visibility: visibility);
      await _remoteDatasource.updateProfileVisibility(requestModel);
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
}

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/entities/user.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/enums/profile_visibility.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/usecases/get_my_profile.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/usecases/update_profile_visibility.dart';

part 'profile_bloc.freezed.dart';
part 'profile_event.dart';
part 'profile_state.dart';

@injectable
class ProfileBloc extends Bloc<ProfileEvent, ProfileState> {
  final GetMyProfile _getMyProfile;
  final UpdateProfileVisibility _updateProfileVisibility;

  ProfileBloc(
    this._getMyProfile,
    this._updateProfileVisibility,
  ) : super(const ProfileState.initial()) {
    on<_LoadMyProfile>(_onLoadMyProfile);
    on<_VisibilityChanged>(_onVisibilityChanged);
  }

  /// Handles fetching the current user's profile.
  Future<void> _onLoadMyProfile(
    _LoadMyProfile event,
    Emitter<ProfileState> emit,
  ) async {
    emit(const ProfileState.loading());
    try {
      final user = await _getMyProfile(NoParams());
      emit(ProfileState.loaded(user: user));
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(ProfileState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ProfileState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  /// Handles updating the user's profile visibility.
  Future<void> _onVisibilityChanged(
    _VisibilityChanged event,
    Emitter<ProfileState> emit,
  ) async {
    // You might want to show a more subtle loading,
    // e.g., by adding a bool to the 'Loaded' state:
    // emit(ProfileState.loaded(user: currentUser, isUpdating: true));
    emit(const ProfileState.loading());

    try {
      final params = UpdateProfileVisibilityParams(
        visibility: event.newVisibility,
      );
      await _updateProfileVisibility(params);

      // On success, dispatch an event to reload the profile
      // to get the updated user object.
      add(const ProfileEvent.loadMyProfile());
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(ProfileState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ProfileState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

abstract mixin class _$LoadedCopyWith<$Res> implements $ProfileStateCopyWith<$Res> {
  factory _$LoadedCopyWith(_Loaded value, $Res Function(_Loaded) _then) = __$LoadedCopyWithImpl;
@useResult
$Res call({
 User user
});




}
/// @nodoc
class __$LoadedCopyWithImpl<$Res>
    implements _$LoadedCopyWith<$Res> {
  __$LoadedCopyWithImpl(this._self, this._then);

  final _Loaded _self;
  final $Res Function(_Loaded) _then;

/// Create a copy of ProfileState
/// with the given fields replaced by the non-null parameter values.
@pragma('vm:prefer-inline') $Res call({Object? user = null,}) {
  return _then(_Loaded(
user: null == user ? _self.user : user // ignore: cast_nullable_to_non_nullable
as User,
  ));
}


}

/// @nodoc


class _Error implements ProfileState {
  const _Error(this.message);
  

 final  String message;

/// Create a copy of ProfileState
/// with the given fields replaced by the non-null parameter values.
@JsonKey(includeFromJson: false, includeToJson: false)
@pragma('vm:prefer-inline')
_$ErrorCopyWith<_Error> get copyWith => __$ErrorCopyWithImpl<_Error>(this, _$identity);



@override
bool operator ==(Object other) {
  return identical(this, other) || (other.runtimeType == runtimeType&&other is _Error&&(identical(other.message, message) || other.message == message));
}


@override
int get hashCode => Object.hash(runtimeType,message);

@override
String toString() {
  return 'ProfileState.error(message: $message)';
}


}

part of 'profile_bloc.dart';

@freezed
sealed class ProfileEvent with _$ProfileEvent {
  /// Dispatched to fetch the current user's profile (e.g., when the page loads).
  const factory ProfileEvent.loadMyProfile() = _LoadMyProfile;

  /// Dispatched when the user changes their visibility setting.
  const factory ProfileEvent.visibilityChanged({
    required ProfileVisibility newVisibility,
  }) = _VisibilityChanged;
}

part of 'profile_bloc.dart';

@freezed
sealed class ProfileState with _$ProfileState {
  /// The initial state, before the profile has been loaded.
  const factory ProfileState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// The state while the profile is being fetched or updated.
  const factory ProfileState.loading() = _Loading;

  /// The state when the user's profile has been successfully loaded.
  const factory ProfileState.loaded({required User user}) = _Loaded;

  /// The state when an error occurred while fetching or updating.
  const factory ProfileState.error(String message) = _Error;
}

import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/entities/user.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/usecases/get_user_profile.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/presentation/bloc/profile_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/presentation/widgets/block_user_button.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/presentation/widgets/profile_header.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/presentation/widgets/user_badge_list.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/presentation/widgets/visibility_settings_widget.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/app_bars/custom_app_bar.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/states/loading_states.dart';

@RoutePage()
class ProfilePage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String? userId;

  const ProfilePage({
    @PathParam('userId') this.userId,
    super.key,
  });

  @override
  State<ProfilePage> createState() => _ProfilePageState();
}

class _ProfilePageState extends State<ProfilePage> {
  bool get _isMyProfile => widget.userId == null;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: CustomAppBar(
        title: _isMyProfile ? 'My Profile' : 'User Profile',
        actions: _isMyProfile
            ? [
                IconButton(
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.settings),
                  onPressed: () {
                    // Navigate to settings
                  },
                ),
              ]
            : null,
      ),
      body: _isMyProfile
          // If it's my profile, use the dedicated ProfileBloc
          ? _MyProfileView()
          // If it's another user's profile, use a FutureBuilder
          : _UserProfileView(userId: widget.userId!),
    );
  }
}

// View for showing the current authenticated user's profile
class _MyProfileView extends StatelessWidget {
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return BlocProvider(
      create: (context) =>
          getIt<ProfileBloc>()..add(const ProfileEvent.loadMyProfile()),
      child: BlocConsumer<ProfileBloc, ProfileState>(
        listener: (context, state) {
          state.maybeWhen(
            error: (message) {
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(
                  content: Text(message),
                  backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
                ),
              );
            },
            orElse: () {},
          );
        },
        builder: (context, state) {
          return state.when(
            initial: () => const LoadingIndicator(),
            loading: () => const LoadingIndicator(),
            loaded: (user) => _ProfileContent(user: user, isMyProfile: true),
            error: (message) => ErrorStateWidget(
              message: message,
              buttonText: 'Retry',
              onRetry: () {
                context
                    .read<ProfileBloc>()
                    .add(const ProfileEvent.loadMyProfile());
              },
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

// View for showing another user's profile
class _UserProfileView extends StatelessWidget {
  final String userId;
  const _UserProfileView({required this.userId});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return FutureBuilder<User>(
      // Fetch the user profile directly with the use case
      future: getIt<GetUserProfile>().call(GetUserProfileParams(userId: userId)),
      builder: (context, snapshot) {
        if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
          return const LoadingIndicator();
        }
        if (snapshot.hasError) {
          return ErrorStateWidget(
            message: snapshot.error.toString(),
            buttonText: 'Go Back',
            onRetry: () => context.router.pop(),
          );
        }
        if (snapshot.hasData) {
          return _ProfileContent(user: snapshot.data!, isMyProfile: false);
        }
        return const Center(child: Text('User not found.'));
      },
    );
  }
}

// Shared content widget for both profile views
class _ProfileContent extends StatelessWidget {
  final User user;
  final bool isMyProfile;

  const _ProfileContent({
    required this.user,
    required this.isMyProfile,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return RefreshIndicator(
      onRefresh: () async {
        if (isMyProfile) {
          context
              .read<ProfileBloc>()
              .add(const ProfileEvent.loadMyProfile());
        }
        // Note: Can't easily refresh the FutureBuilder,
        // this is a limitation of this approach.
      },
      child: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AppTheme.defaultPadding),
        children: [
          // 1. Header
          ProfileHeader(user: user),

          // 2. Badges
          UserBadgeList(badges: user.userBadges),
          const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),

          // 3. Settings (My Profile) or Block (Other Profile)
          if (isMyProfile)
            VisibilitySettingsWidget(
              currentVisibility: user.profileVisibility,
              verifiedGender: user.verifiedGender,
              onChanged: (newVisibility) {
                // Dispatch event to update visibility
                context.read<ProfileBloc>().add(
                      ProfileEvent.visibilityChanged(
                          newVisibility: newVisibility),
                    );
              },
            )
          else
            BlockUserButton(
              userId: user.userId,
              userName: user.firstName,
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';

          currentVisibility: user.profileVisibility,
          verifiedGender: user.verifiedGender,
          onChanged: (newVisibility) {

                .read<ProfileBloc>()
                .add(ProfileEvent.visibilityChanged(newVisibility: newVisibility));
          },
        ),
        const Divider(),

          leading: const Icon(Icons.rate_review_outlined),
          title: const Text('Pending Ratings'),
          trailing: const Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios_rounded, size: 16),
          onTap: () {
            context.push(const PendingRatingsRoute());

        ),
        ListTile(
          leading: const Icon(Icons.add_business_outlined),
          title: const Text('Submit a New Gym'),
          trailing: const Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios_rounded, size: 16),

            context.push(const SubmitGymRoute());

        //     // context.push(const BlockedUsersRoute());
        //   },

        const Divider(),

        // --- Account Actions ---

          leading: Icon(Icons.logout, color: AppTheme.primaryRed),
          title: const Text('Log Out'),
          textColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
          onTap: () => _showLogoutDialog(context),

      context: context,
      builder: (_) => ConfirmationDialog(
        title: 'Log Out',
        message: 'Are you sure you want to log out?',
        confirmText: 'Log Out',
        cancelText: 'Cancel',
        onConfirm: () {
          // Use getIt to find the global AppBloc and dispatch the event

import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/di/service_locator.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/usecases/block_user.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/dialogs/custom_dialogs.dart';

class BlockUserButton extends StatefulWidget {
  final String userId;
  final String userName;

  const BlockUserButton({
    super.key,
    required this.userId,
    required this.userName,
  });

  @override
  State<BlockUserButton> createState() => _BlockUserButtonState();
}

class _BlockUserButtonState extends State<BlockUserButton> {
  bool _isLoading = false;

  Future<void> _handleBlock() async {
    // 1. Show Confirmation Dialog
    final bool? didConfirm = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => ConfirmationDialog(
        title: 'Block ${widget.userName}?',
        message:
            'Are you sure you want to block this user? You will no longer see their sessions, and they will not see yours.',
        confirmText: 'Block',
        cancelText: 'Cancel',
        onConfirm: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true),
      ),
    );

    if (didConfirm != true) return;

    // 2. Perform Block Action
    setState(() => _isLoading = true);
    try {
      await getIt<BlockUser>().call(BlockUserParams(userId: widget.userId));

      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text('${widget.userName} has been blocked.'),
            backgroundColor: AppTheme.successGreen,
          ),
        );
        // Pop back from the profile
        context.router.pop();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text('Failed to block user: $e'),
            backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
          ),
        );
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SizedBox(
      width: double.infinity,
      child: TextButton(
        onPressed: _isLoading ? null : _handleBlock,
        style: TextButton.styleFrom(
          foregroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
        ),
        child: _isLoading
            ? const SizedBox(
                height: 20,
                width: 20,
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                  strokeWidth: 2,
                  color: AppTheme.primaryRed,
                ),
              )
            : Text('Block ${widget.userName}'),
      ),
    );
  }
}
