import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/entities/user.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/enums/user_status.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/enums/verification_status_enum.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/badges/custom_badges.dart';

class ProfileHeader extends StatelessWidget {
  final User user;

  const ProfileHeader({
    super.key,
    required this.user,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        // Profile Photo
        CircleAvatar(
          radius: 50,
          backgroundColor: AppTheme.lightGray,
          backgroundImage: user.profilePhotoUrl != null
              ? NetworkImage(user.profilePhotoUrl!)
              : null,
          child: user.profilePhotoUrl == null
              ? const Icon(Icons.person, size: 50, color: AppTheme.disabledGray)
              : null,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.elementSpacing),

        // User Name
        Text(
          user.firstName,
          style: context.textTheme.displaySmall,
          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.elementSpacing),

        // Status Badges
        _buildStatusBadges(context, user),
        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),

        // Primary Gym
        if (user.primaryGym != null)
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              const Icon(Icons.location_on_outlined,
                  size: 16, color: AppTheme.disabledGray),
              const SizedBox(width: 4),
              Text(
                user.primaryGym!.name,
                style: context.textTheme.bodyMedium
                    ?.copyWith(color: AppTheme.darkGray),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),

        // User Stats
        Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceEvenly,
          children: [
            _buildStatColumn(
              context,
              Icons.groups_rounded,
              'Sessions',
              user.groupSessionCount.toString(),
            ),
            _buildStatColumn(
              context,
              Icons.event_busy_rounded,
              'No-Shows',
              user.noShowCount.toString(),
            ),
            _buildStatColumn(
              context,
              Icons.star_rounded,
              'Rating',
              'N/A', // Assuming rating isn't part of User entity yet
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.sectionSpacing),
        const Divider(),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatusBadges(BuildContext context, User user) {
    return Wrap(
      spacing: AppTheme.elementSpacing,
      runSpacing: AppTheme.elementSpacing,
      alignment: WrapAlignment.center,
      children: [
        if (user.verificationStatus == VerificationStatus.verified)
          StatusBadge.verified(),
        if (user.verificationStatus == VerificationStatus.pendingReview)
          StatusBadge.probationary(),
        if (user.status == UserStatus.suspended) StatusBadge.unreliable(),
        // TODO: Add 'Reliable' badge based on logic
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatColumn(
      BuildContext context, IconData icon, String label, String value) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Text(
          value,
          style: context.textTheme.headlineMedium,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        Row(
          children: [
            Icon(icon, size: 14, color: AppTheme.disabledGray),
            const SizedBox(width: 4),
            Text(
              label,
              style: context.textTheme.bodySmall
                  ?.copyWith(color: AppTheme.disabledGray),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart' hide Badge;
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/entities/badge.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/states/loading_states.dart'; // For EmptyStateWidget

class UserBadgeList extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<Badge> badges;

  const UserBadgeList({
    super.key,
    required this.badges,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (badges.isEmpty) {
      return const EmptyStateWidget(
        icon: Icons.shield_outlined,
        title: 'No Badges Yet',
        message: 'Badges you earn from sessions will appear here.',
      );
    }

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
            horizontal: AppTheme.defaultPadding,
          ),
          child: Text(
            'Badges',
            style: context.textTheme.titleLarge,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.elementSpacing),
        SizedBox(
          height: 100, // Fixed height for horizontal list
          child: ListView.separated(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
              horizontal: AppTheme.defaultPadding,
            ),
            scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
            itemCount: badges.length,
            separatorBuilder: (context, index) =>
                const SizedBox(width: AppTheme.elementSpacing),
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final badge = badges[index];
              return Tooltip(
                message: badge.description,
                child: SizedBox(
                  width: 80,
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      CircleAvatar(
                        radius: 30,
                        backgroundColor: AppTheme.lightGray,
                        // Use Image.network if iconUrl is present, else placeholder
                        child: badge.iconUrl != null
                            ? Image.network(
                                badge.iconUrl!,
                                fit: BoxFit.cover,
                                errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) =>
                                    const Icon(Icons.shield,
                                        color: AppTheme.disabledGray),
                              )
                            : const Icon(Icons.shield,
                                color: AppTheme.disabledGray),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text(
                        badge.badgeName,
                        style: context.textTheme.bodySmall,
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        maxLines: 2,
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              );
            },
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/user/domain/enums/profile_visibility.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/enums/verified_gender_enum.dart';

class VisibilitySettingsWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  final ProfileVisibility currentVisibility;
  final VerifiedGender verifiedGender;
  final ValueChanged<ProfileVisibility> onChanged;

  const VisibilitySettingsWidget({
    super.key,
    required this.currentVisibility,
    required this.verifiedGender,
    required this.onChanged,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final bool canSelectSameGender =
        verifiedGender != VerifiedGender.notVerified;

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        horizontal: AppTheme.defaultPadding,
        vertical: AppTheme.elementSpacing,
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            'Profile Visibility',
            style: context.textTheme.titleLarge,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.elementSpacing),
          Text(
            'Control who can see your profile and sessions.',
            style: context.textTheme.bodyMedium,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AppTheme.elementSpacing),
          // Option 1: All Users
          RadioListTile<ProfileVisibility>(
            title: const Text('Show to all users'),
            value: ProfileVisibility.all,
            groupValue: currentVisibility,
            onChanged: (value) => onChanged(ProfileVisibility.all),
            activeColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
          ),
          // Option 2: Same Gender Only
          RadioListTile<ProfileVisibility>(
            title: const Text('Show to same gender only'),
            subtitle: !canSelectSameGender
                ? Text(
                    'You must be verified to use this option.',
                    style: context.textTheme.bodySmall
                        ?.copyWith(color: AppTheme.disabledGray),
                  )
                : null,
            value: ProfileVisibility.sameGender,
            groupValue: currentVisibility,
            // Disable if gender is not verified
            onChanged: canSelectSameGender
                ? (value) => onChanged(ProfileVisibility.sameGender)
                : null,
            activeColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:io';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';

import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/data/models/verification_status_model.dart';
import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

abstract class VerificationRemoteDatasource {
  Future<VerificationStatusModel> getVerificationStatus();
  Future<void> submitVerificationVideo(File video);
}

@Injectable(as: VerificationRemoteDatasource)
class VerificationRemoteDatasourceImpl implements VerificationRemoteDatasource {
  final ApiClient _apiClient;

  VerificationRemoteDatasourceImpl(this._apiClient);

  @override
  Future<VerificationStatusModel> getVerificationStatus() async {
    try {
      final response = await _apiClient.dio.get(
        ApiEndpoints.verificationStatus,
      );
      return VerificationStatusModel.fromJson(response.data);
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> submitVerificationVideo(File video) async {
    try {
      // 1. Get the file name
      String fileName = p.basename(video.path);

      // 2. Create the FormData object
      // This is the multipart request body
      FormData formData = FormData.fromMap({
        // The key "video" MUST match the @RequestParam("video")
        // in your VerificationController.java
        "video": await MultipartFile.fromFile(
          video.path,
          filename: fileName,
        ),
      });

      // 3. Send the request
      await _apiClient.dio.post(
        ApiEndpoints.submitVerification,
        data: formData,
        // Optional: Show upload progress
        onSendProgress: (int sent, int total) {
          print('Upload progress: ${((sent / total) * 100).toStringAsFixed(0)}%');
        },
      );
      // Backend returns a 200 OK with a simple string, which we ignore
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      throw ServerException.fromDio(e);
    }
  }
}

      _$VerificationStatusModelFromJson(json);

  // Converts the data-layer model to a domain-layer entity
  VerificationStatusEntity toEntity() {

      status: status,
      gender: gender,
    );
  }

import 'dart:io';

import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/repositories/verification_repository.dart';

@Injectable(as: VerificationRepository)
class VerificationRepositoryImpl implements VerificationRepository {
  final VerificationRemoteDatasource _remoteDatasource;

  VerificationRepositoryImpl(this._remoteDatasource);

  @override
  Future<VerificationStatusEntity> getVerificationStatus() async {
    try {
      final statusModel = await _remoteDatasource.getVerificationStatus();
      return statusModel.toEntity();
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> submitVerificationVideo(File video) async {
    try {
      await _remoteDatasource.submitVerificationVideo(video);
    } on ServerException catch (e) {
      throw ServerFailure(message: e.message);
    }
  }
}

import 'dart:io';

import 'package:bloc/bloc.dart';
import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
import 'package:injectable/injectable.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/errors/failure.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/usecases/usecase.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/entities/verification_status_entity.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/usecases/get_verification_status.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/usecases/submit_verification_video.dart';

part 'verification_cubit.freezed.dart';
part 'verification_state.dart';

@injectable
class VerificationCubit extends Cubit<VerificationState> {
  final GetVerificationStatus _getVerificationStatus;
  final SubmitVerificationVideo _submitVerificationVideo;

  VerificationCubit(
    this._getVerificationStatus,
    this._submitVerificationVideo,
  ) : super(const VerificationState.initial());

  /// Fetches the user's current verification status from the server.
  /// This is called from main.dart on app start.
  Future<void> fetchStatus() async {
    emit(const VerificationState.loading());
    try {
      final status = await _getVerificationStatus(NoParams());
      emit(VerificationState.loaded(status));
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(VerificationState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(VerificationState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  /// Submits the verification video.
  /// On success, it immediately re-fetches the status to update
  /// the state from 'not_started' to 'pending_review'.
  Future<void> submitVideo(File video) async {
    emit(const VerificationState.loading());
    try {
      final params = SubmitVerificationVideoParams(video: video);
      await _submitVerificationVideo(params);

      // On success, refresh the status
      await fetchStatus();
    } on ServerFailure catch (e) {
      emit(VerificationState.error(e.message));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(VerificationState.error(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

/// A variant of `when` that fallback to returning `null`
///
/// It is equivalent to doing:
/// ```dart
/// switch (sealedClass) {
///   case Subclass(:final field):
///     return ...;
///   case _:
///     return null;
/// }
/// ```

@optionalTypeArgs TResult? whenOrNull<TResult extends Object?>({TResult? Function()?  initial,TResult? Function()?  loading,TResult? Function( VerificationStatusEntity status)?  loaded,TResult? Function( String message)?  error,}) {final _that = this;
switch (_that) {
case _Initial() when initial != null:
return initial();case _Loading() when loading != null:
return loading();case _Loaded() when loaded != null:
return loaded(_that.status);case _Error() when error != null:
return error(_that.message);case _:
  return null;

}
}

}

  const _Initial();
  






@override
bool operator ==(Object other) {
  return identical(this, other) || (other.runtimeType == runtimeType&&other is _Initial);
}


@override
int get hashCode => runtimeType.hashCode;

@override
String toString() {
  return 'VerificationState.initial()';
}


}

}

@override
String toString() {

}


}

part of 'verification_cubit.dart';

@freezed
sealed class VerificationState with _$VerificationState {
  /// We haven't fetched the status yet.
  const factory VerificationState.initial() = _Initial;

  /// We are currently fetching the status.
  const factory VerificationState.loading() = _Loading;

  /// We have successfully fetched the status.
  const factory VerificationState.loaded(VerificationStatusEntity status) = _Loaded;

  /// An error occurred.
  const factory VerificationState.error(String message) = _Error;
}

import 'dart:io';
import 'package:auto_route/auto_route.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_router.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/presentation/cubit/verification_cubit.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/presentation/widgets/video_recorder_widget.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/app_bars/custom_app_bar.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/states/loading_states.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/domain/enums/verification_status_enum.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/features/verification/presentation/widgets/verification_success_dialog.dart';

@RoutePage()
class SubmitVerificationPage extends StatelessWidget {
  const SubmitVerificationPage({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final textTheme = Theme.of(context).textTheme;

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: const CustomAppBar(title: 'Verify Your Profile'),
      body: BlocConsumer<VerificationCubit, VerificationState>(
        listener: (context, state) {
          state.maybeWhen(
            loaded: (status) {
              // When submission is successful, the cubit refetches.
              // If the new status is pending, show success and pop.
              if (status.status == VerificationStatus.pendingReview) {
                // Show the success dialog
                VerificationSuccessDialog.show(context);
                // Pop this page
                context.router.pop();
              }
            },
            error: (message) {
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(
                  content: Text(message),
                  backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error,
                ),
              );
            },
            orElse: () {},
          );
        },
        builder: (context, state) {
          return state.maybeWhen(
            loading: () =>
                const LoadingIndicator(message: 'Submitting your video...'),
            orElse: () => _buildInstructions(context, textTheme),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildInstructions(BuildContext context, TextTheme textTheme) {
    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            'Start Verification',
            style: textTheme.headlineSmall,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            'To keep our community safe, please record a short 5-second video.',
            style: textTheme.bodyMedium,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary.withOpacity(0.05),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
              border: Border.all(
                color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary.withOpacity(0.2),
              ),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Please say the following phrase clearly:',
                  style: textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(
                  '"Hi, my name is [Your Name] and today is [Today\'s Date]."',
                  style: textTheme.bodyLarge?.copyWith(
                    fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(
                  'After, please turn your head to the side.',
                  style: textTheme.bodyMedium,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          VideoRecorderWidget(
            onVideoRecorded: (File videoFile) {
              // Call the cubit to submit the video
              context.read<VerificationCubit>().submitVideo(videoFile);
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// C:\Users\Ahmed\Desktop\sahbifit\mobile\lib\features\verification\presentation\widgets\video_recorder_widget.dart

import 'dart:io';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:camera/camera.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/buttons/custom_buttons.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/states/loading_states.dart';
import 'package:video_player/video_player.dart';

class VideoRecorderWidget extends StatefulWidget {
  final Function(File videoFile) onVideoRecorded;

  const VideoRecorderWidget({
    super.key,
    required this.onVideoRecorded,
  });

  @override
  State<VideoRecorderWidget> createState() => _VideoRecorderWidgetState();
}

class _VideoRecorderWidgetState extends State<VideoRecorderWidget> {
  CameraController? _controller;
  VideoPlayerController? _videoPlayerController;
  bool _isInitialized = false;
  bool _isRecording = false;
  String? _videoPath;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _initializeCamera();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller?.dispose();
    _videoPlayerController?.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _initializeCamera() async {
    try {
      final cameras = await availableCameras();
      // Find the front camera
      final frontCamera = cameras.firstWhere(
        (camera) => camera.lensDirection == CameraLensDirection.front,
        orElse: () => cameras.first,
      );

      _controller = CameraController(
        frontCamera,
        ResolutionPreset.medium,
        enableAudio: true,
      );

      await _controller!.initialize();
      setState(() {
        _isInitialized = true;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      print("Error initializing camera: $e");
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(content: Text('Failed to initialize camera.')),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _toggleRecording() async {
    if (!_isInitialized || _controller == null) return;

    if (_isRecording) {
      // Stop recording
      try {
        final file = await _controller!.stopVideoRecording();
        setState(() {
          _isRecording = false;
          _videoPath = file.path;
        });
        _initializeVideoPlayer();
      } catch (e) {
        print("Error stopping video: $e");
      }
    } else {
      // Start recording
      try {
        await _controller!.startVideoRecording();
        setState(() {
          _isRecording = true;
        });
      } catch (e) {
        print("Error starting video: $e");
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _initializeVideoPlayer() async {
    if (_videoPath == null) return;
    _videoPlayerController = VideoPlayerController.file(File(_videoPath!));
    await _videoPlayerController!.initialize();
    await _videoPlayerController!.setLooping(true);
    await _videoPlayerController!.play();
    setState(() {});
  }

  void _reset() {
    _videoPlayerController?.dispose();
    setState(() {
      _videoPath = null;
      _videoPlayerController = null;
      _isRecording = false;
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_videoPath != null && _videoPlayerController != null) {
      return _buildPreview();
    }

    if (!_isInitialized || _controller == null) {
      return const AspectRatio(
        aspectRatio: 9 / 16,
        child: LoadingIndicator(message: 'Loading camera...'),
      );
    }

    return _buildRecorder();
  }

  Widget _buildRecorder() {
    return Column(
      children: [
        ClipRRect(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          child: AspectRatio(
            aspectRatio: 9 / 16,
            child: CameraPreview(_controller!),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        FloatingActionButton(
          onPressed: _toggleRecording,
          backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
          child: Icon(
            _isRecording ? Icons.stop_rounded : Icons.videocam_rounded,
            color: AppTheme.cleanWhite,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPreview() {
    return Column(
      children: [
        ClipRRect(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          child: AspectRatio(
            aspectRatio: 9 / 16,
            child: _videoPlayerController!.value.isInitialized
                ? VideoPlayer(_videoPlayerController!)
                : const LoadingIndicator(message: 'Loading preview...'),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: SecondaryButton(
                text: 'Retake',
                onPressed: _reset,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 16),
            Expanded(
              child: PrimaryButton(
                text: 'Submit',
                onPressed: () {
                  widget.onVideoRecorded(File(_videoPath!));
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        )
      ],
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';

class CustomAppBar extends StatelessWidget implements PreferredSizeWidget {
  final String title;
  final List<Widget>? actions;
  final bool showBackButton;
  final VoidCallback? onBackPressed;
  final Widget? leading;

  const CustomAppBar({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    this.actions,
    this.showBackButton = true,
    this.onBackPressed,
    this.leading,
  });

  @override
  Size get preferredSize => const Size.fromHeight(kToolbarHeight);

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AppBar(
      title: Text(
        title,
        style: Theme.of(context).appBarTheme.titleTextStyle,
      ),
      leading: showBackButton
          ? (leading ??
              IconButton(
                icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back_ios_rounded),
                onPressed: onBackPressed ?? () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
              ))
          : null,
      actions: actions,
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.cleanWhite,
      foregroundColor: AppTheme.darkGray,
      elevation: 0,
      centerTitle: false,
    );
  }
}

class TransparentAppBar extends StatelessWidget implements PreferredSizeWidget {
  final String title;
  final List<Widget>? actions;
  final bool showBackButton;
  final Color? backgroundColor;

  const TransparentAppBar({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    this.actions,
    this.showBackButton = true,
    this.backgroundColor,
  });

  @override
  Size get preferredSize => const Size.fromHeight(kToolbarHeight);

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AppBar(
      title: Text(
        title,
        style: Theme.of(context).appBarTheme.titleTextStyle,
      ),
      leading: showBackButton
          ? IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back_ios_rounded),
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
            )
          : null,
      actions: actions,
      backgroundColor: backgroundColor ?? Colors.transparent,
      foregroundColor: AppTheme.darkGray,
      elevation: 0,
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart'; // Assuming AppTheme is correctly imported

class StatusBadge extends StatelessWidget {
  final String text;
  final Color backgroundColor;
  final Color textColor;

  const StatusBadge({
    super.key,
    required this.text,
    required this.backgroundColor,
    required this.textColor,
  });

  factory StatusBadge.verified() {
    return StatusBadge(
      text: 'Verified',
      // Replaced .withOpacity(0.1) with .withAlpha(26)
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.trustBlue.withAlpha(26),
      textColor: AppTheme.trustBlue,
    );
  }

  factory StatusBadge.probationary() {
    return StatusBadge(
      text: 'Under Review',
      // Replaced .withOpacity(0.1) with .withAlpha(26)
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.warningYellow.withAlpha(26),
      textColor: AppTheme.darkGray,
    );
  }

  factory StatusBadge.reliable() {
    return StatusBadge(
      text: 'Reliable',
      // Replaced .withOpacity(0.1) with .withAlpha(26)
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.successGreen.withAlpha(26),
      textColor: AppTheme.successGreen,
    );
  }

  factory StatusBadge.unreliable() {
    return StatusBadge(
      text: 'Unreliable',
      // Replaced .withOpacity(0.1) with .withAlpha(26)
      backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed.withAlpha(26),
      textColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: backgroundColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        // Replaced .withOpacity(0.3) with .withAlpha(77)
        border: Border.all(color: textColor.withAlpha(77)),
      ),
      child: Text(
        text,
        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelSmall?.copyWith(
              color: textColor,
              fontSize: 10,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
            ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class TagChip extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final bool selected;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const TagChip({
    super.key,
    required this.label,
    required this.selected,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: selected ? AppTheme.primaryRed : AppTheme.lightGray,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        ),
        child: Text(
          label,
          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                color: selected ? AppTheme.cleanWhite : AppTheme.darkGray,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
              ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';

class PrimaryButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final String text;
  final VoidCallback onPressed;
  final bool isLoading;
  final bool enabled;
  final double? width;

  const PrimaryButton({
    super.key,
    required this.text,
    required this.onPressed,
    this.isLoading = false,
    this.enabled = true,
    this.width,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SizedBox(
      width: width ?? double.infinity,
      child: ElevatedButton(
        onPressed: enabled && !isLoading ? onPressed : null,
        style: AppTheme.primaryButtonStyle,
        child: isLoading
            ? SizedBox(
                height: 20,
                width: 20,
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                  strokeWidth: 2,
                  valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(
                    AppTheme.cleanWhite,
                  ),
                ),
              )
            : Text(
                text,
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelLarge,
              ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class SecondaryButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final String text;
  final VoidCallback onPressed;
  final bool isLoading;
  final bool enabled;
  final double? width;

  const SecondaryButton({
    super.key,
    required this.text,
    required this.onPressed,
    this.isLoading = false,
    this.enabled = true,
    this.width,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SizedBox(
      width: width ?? double.infinity,
      child: OutlinedButton(
        onPressed: enabled && !isLoading ? onPressed : null,
        style: AppTheme.secondaryButtonStyle,
        child: isLoading
            ? SizedBox(
                height: 20,
                width: 20,
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                  strokeWidth: 2,
                  valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(
                    AppTheme.darkGray,
                  ),
                ),
              )
            : Text(
                text,
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                      color: AppTheme.darkGray,
                    ),
              ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class TextIconButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final String text;
  final IconData icon;
  final VoidCallback onPressed;
  final Color? color;

  const TextIconButton({
    super.key,
    required this.text,
    required this.icon,
    required this.onPressed,
    this.color,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return TextButton(
      onPressed: onPressed,
      style: TextButton.styleFrom(
        foregroundColor: color ?? AppTheme.primaryRed,
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Icon(icon, size: 18),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Text(text),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart'; // Assuming AppTheme is correctly imported

class PrimaryCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry? padding;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;
  final Color? backgroundColor;

  const PrimaryCard({
    super.key,
    required this.child,
    this.padding,
    this.onTap,
    this.backgroundColor,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Card(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8, horizontal: 16),
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        child: Container(
          padding: padding ?? const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: backgroundColor ?? AppTheme.cleanWhite,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          ),
          child: child,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class SessionCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final String subtitle;
  final String time;
  final String location;
  final int participants;
  final int maxParticipants;
  final bool isLocked;
  final bool isVerified;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;

  const SessionCard({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
    required this.time,
    required this.location,
    required this.participants,
    required this.maxParticipants,
    this.isLocked = false,
    this.isVerified = false,
    this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return PrimaryCard(
      onTap: isLocked ? null : onTap,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  title,
                  style: AppTheme.sessionCardTitle,
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ),
              if (isLocked)
                const Icon(
                  Icons.lock_outline,
                  color: AppTheme.disabledGray,
                  size: 20,
                ),
              if (isVerified)
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                  decoration: AppTheme.verifiedBadgeDecoration,
                  child: Text(
                    'Verified',
                    style: AppTheme.verifiedBadgeText,
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Text(
            subtitle,
            style: AppTheme.sessionCardSubtitle,
            maxLines: 2,
            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Row(
            children: [
              Icon(
                Icons.access_time,
                size: 16,
                // Replaced .withOpacity(0.6) with .withAlpha(153)
                color: AppTheme.darkGray.withAlpha(153),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 4),
              Text(
                time,
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                      // Replaced .withOpacity(0.8) with .withAlpha(204)
                      color: AppTheme.darkGray.withAlpha(204),
                    ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Icon(
                Icons.location_on_outlined,
                size: 16,
                // Replaced .withOpacity(0.6) with .withAlpha(153)
                color: AppTheme.darkGray.withAlpha(153),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 4),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  location,
                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                        // Replaced .withOpacity(0.8) with .withAlpha(204)
                        color: AppTheme.darkGray.withAlpha(204),
                      ),
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Row(
            children: [
              Icon(
                Icons.people_outline,
                size: 16,
                // Replaced .withOpacity(0.6) with .withAlpha(153)
                color: AppTheme.darkGray.withAlpha(153),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 4),
              Text(
                '$participants/$maxParticipants',
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                      // Replaced .withOpacity(0.8) with .withAlpha(204)
                      color: AppTheme.darkGray.withAlpha(204),
                    ),
              ),
              const Spacer(),
              if (isLocked)
                Text(
                  'Verification Required',
                  style: AppTheme.lockedFeatureText,
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/shared/widgets/buttons/custom_buttons.dart';

class ConfirmationDialog extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final String message;
  final String confirmText;
  final String cancelText;
  final VoidCallback onConfirm;
  final VoidCallback? onCancel;

  const ConfirmationDialog({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.message,
    required this.confirmText,
    required this.cancelText,
    required this.onConfirm,
    this.onCancel,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      title: Text(title),
      content: Text(message),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: onCancel ?? () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          child: Text(cancelText),
        ),
        ElevatedButton(
          onPressed: () {
            Navigator.of(context).pop();
            onConfirm();
          },
          style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
            backgroundColor: AppTheme.primaryRed,
            foregroundColor: AppTheme.cleanWhite,
          ),
          child: Text(confirmText),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class InfoBottomSheet extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final String message;
  final String buttonText;
  final VoidCallback onButtonPressed;

  const InfoBottomSheet({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.message,
    required this.buttonText,
    required this.onButtonPressed,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: AppTheme.cleanWhite,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(
          topLeft: Radius.circular(16),
          topRight: Radius.circular(16),
        ),
      ),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            title,
            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          Text(
            message,
            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          PrimaryButton(
            text: buttonText,
            onPressed: onButtonPressed,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';

class PrimaryTextField extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final String? hintText;
  final TextEditingController? controller;
  final TextInputType keyboardType;
  final bool obscureText;
  final bool enabled;
  final String? errorText;
  final int? maxLines;
  final int? maxLength;
  final Widget? suffixIcon;
  final Widget? prefixIcon;
  final ValueChanged<String>? onChanged;
  final FormFieldValidator<String>? validator;
  final bool readOnly; // <-- ADDED
  final VoidCallback? onTap; // <-- ADDED

  const PrimaryTextField({
    super.key,
    required this.label,
    this.hintText,
    this.controller,
    this.keyboardType = TextInputType.text,
    this.obscureText = false,
    this.enabled = true,
    this.errorText,
    this.maxLines = 1,
    this.maxLength,
    this.suffixIcon,
    this.prefixIcon,
    this.onChanged,
    this.validator,
    this.readOnly = false, // <-- ADDED
    this.onTap, // <-- ADDED
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return TextFormField(
      controller: controller,
      keyboardType: keyboardType,
      obscureText: obscureText,
      enabled: enabled,
      maxLines: maxLines,
      maxLength: maxLength,
      onChanged: onChanged,
      validator: validator,
      readOnly: readOnly, // <-- ADDED
      onTap: onTap, // <-- ADDED
      decoration: InputDecoration(
        labelText: label,
        hintText: hintText,
        suffixIcon: suffixIcon,
        prefixIcon: prefixIcon,
        errorText: errorText,
      ),
    );
  }
}

class SearchTextField extends StatelessWidget {
  final String hintText;
  final ValueChanged<String>? onChanged;
  final TextEditingController? controller;

  const SearchTextField({
    super.key,
    required this.hintText,
    this.onChanged,
    this.controller,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return TextField(
      controller: controller,
      onChanged: onChanged,
      decoration: InputDecoration(
        hintText: hintText,
        prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search),
        filled: true,
        fillColor: AppTheme.lightGray,
        border: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: BorderSide.none,
        ),
        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: BorderSide.none,
        ),
        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppTheme.primaryRed, width: 2),
        ),
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sahbifit/core/config/app_theme.dart';

class LoadingIndicator extends StatelessWidget {
  final String? message;

  const LoadingIndicator({super.key, this.message});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Center(
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          const CircularProgressIndicator(
            valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(AppTheme.primaryRed),
          ),
          if (message != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Text(
              message!,
              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class EmptyStateWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final String message;
  final String? buttonText;
  final VoidCallback? onButtonPressed;
  final IconData icon;

  const EmptyStateWidget({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.message,
    this.buttonText,
    this.onButtonPressed,
    this.icon = Icons.fitness_center_outlined,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32.0),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Icon(
            icon,
            size: 64,
            color: AppTheme.lightGray,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          Text(
            title,
            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            message,
            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          ),
          if (buttonText != null && onButtonPressed != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            SizedBox(
              width: 200,
              child: ElevatedButton(
                onPressed: onButtonPressed,
                style: AppTheme.primaryButtonStyle,
                child: Text(buttonText!),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class ErrorStateWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  final String message;
  final String buttonText;
  final VoidCallback onRetry;

  const ErrorStateWidget({
    super.key,
    required this.message,
    required this.buttonText,
    required this.onRetry,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32.0),

            Icons.error_outline,

          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          Text(
            'Something went wrong',
            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            message,
            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          SizedBox(
            width: 200,
            child: ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: onRetry,
              style: AppTheme.primaryButtonStyle,
              child: Text(buttonText),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
